A strength-2 covering-array sampler controls nine generation axes: domain, format, difficulty, length, density, code-switching, language, adjacency, and co-occurrence. Three entity-level metadata fields (disclosure status, disclosure form, and a GDPR-aligned sensitivity tier) enable stratified evaluation beyond aggregate or per-type F1. From the full benchmark, we evaluate five detectors (Presidio, GLiNER, the OpenAI Privacy Filter, GPT-4.1, and Claude Sonnet 4.6) on a locked, language-stratified sample of 1,000 records. Aggregate F1 masks an architecture-dependent failure structure: the rule-based detector performs poorly on the highest-stakes data, including HIGH-sensitivity categories (recall 0.07) and non-verbatim disclosure forms, while the LLM detectors remain more robust, with the HIGH tier as their strongest sensitivity slice. A three-model reference-free LLM-as-judge assessment corroborates that sensitivity-tier assignment is the task's hardest axis. We release the benchmark, schema, prompts, and stratified evaluation harness.

</details>


### [139] [Light-weight Pronunciation Assessment via Discrete Speech Token Surprisal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19910)
*Syeda Faiza Ahmed Sara,Shammur Absar Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级发音评估框架，仅用母语音频训练，通过SSL编码+K-means离散化获取声学token，利用母语token语言模型计算 surprisal 作为音位偏离度量，并结合Text2DUnit-DTW对齐模块融合转录指导特征，在无监督/轻校准设定下逼近有监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统发音评估依赖标注的二语学习者错误数据或非母语语料库，采集成本高昂。本文旨在探索仅使用易获取的母语语音资源，以无监督或极少标注样本校准的方式，实现有效的发音质量评估。

Method: 1) 用SSL编码器（如Wav2Vec 2.0）提取学习者语音特征，经K-means码本离散化为声学token序列；2) 在母语token序列上训练语言模型，推理时计算学习者token序列的surprisal（高surprisal=音位偏离）；3) 引入Text2DUnit-DTW模块：从参考文本预测母语token序列，与声学token进行DTW对齐，提取错误敏感特征；4) 将surprisal特征与对齐特征通过简单回归融合，输出发音评分。

Result: 在SpeechOcean762数据集上，Pearson相关系数从纯surprisal的0.60提升至加入转录指导后的0.66，接近有监督基线；在L2-ARCTIC跨数据集评测中同样表现出一致的增益。

Conclusion: 仅依赖母语语音资源即可构建有效的发音评估系统，Text2DUnit-DTW对齐模块的引入显著提升了与人工评分的相关性，验证了该轻量级框架的可行性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Training automated pronunciation assessment often relies on labeled learner errors or non-native corpora that are costly to collect. We propose a lightweight framework trained only on native speech resources, operating unsupervised or lightly calibrated with a small set of scored utterances. At inference, learner speech is discretized with an SSL encoder and a K-means codebook. A token language model trained on native sequences computes surprisal where higher surprisal indicates phonotactic deviation. We add a transcript-guided Text2DUnit--DTW module that predicts native token sequences from reference text and aligns them to acoustic tokens to derive error-sensitive features. Surprisal and alignment features are fused via simple regression. On SpeechOcean762, PCC improves from 0.60 to 0.66 with transcript guidance, near supervised baselines. Cross-dataset evaluation on L2-ARCTIC shows consistent gains.

</details>


### [140] [GEMS: Geometric Constraints Enable Multi-Semantic Superposition in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19946)
*Yu Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了训练免多方向激活操控中的模型崩塌问题，将其分解为分布偏差和方向干扰两个独立因素，并提出GEMS方法，通过范数保持加权叠加和实时正交化分别解决这两个问题，实现多方向的同时操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活操控方法仅支持单方向注入；当多个语义方向叠加时，模型性能会崩溃（如GSM8K从92%降至4%）。需要理解崩溃的成因，并设计无需重新训练的多方向操控方法。

Method: 首先将多方向注入的崩溃分解为两个独立因素：(1) 分布偏差——加性扰动跨层累积导致激活偏离训练分布；(2) 方向干扰——非正交语义向量叠加时互相削弱。基于此提出GEMS方法：针对分布偏差采用范数保持的加权叠加和定向注意力通路注入；针对方向干扰采用实时正交化。通过组件消融和层级探针验证各约束的因果作用。

Result: 在GSM8K上同时注入三个非数学方向，GEMS保持98%准确率（基线92%），而无约束叠加降至4%；在Wikitext-2上，同样注入仅增加2.2%困惑度。消融实验确认了每个约束的因果贡献，层级探针证实正交化信号可通过FFN通路并保持语义特异性到达输出分布。操控效果可在3B到31B不同架构间迁移。

Conclusion: 多方向激活操控的崩溃可分解为分布偏差和方向干扰两个独立因素；GEMS通过几何约束有效解决该问题，为训练免的多方向模型行为控制提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Activation steering controls model behavior by modifying intermediate hidden states at inference time without retraining. Existing methods handle only single-direction injection; when multiple semantic directions are superposed without constraints, the model collapses. We show that this collapse decomposes into two independently acting sources: distributional deviation, where additive perturbations accumulate in norm across layers and drive activations outside the training distribution, and directional interference, where non-orthogonal semantic vectors mutually dampen when superposed. These two sources define the design constraints that any training-free multi-directional intervention must address. As one instantiation of these principles, we propose GEMS, a training-free method that maps each source to a corresponding geometric constraint: norm-preserving weighted superposition and targeted attention-pathway injection for distributional deviation, and real-time orthogonalization for directional interference. On GSM8K, injecting three concurrent non-mathematical directions preserves accuracy at 98% (baseline 92%), while unconstrained addition collapses to 4%; on Wikitext-2, the same injection incurs only 2.2% PPL increase. Component ablation isolates the causal role of each constraint, and layer-level probes confirm that orthogonalized signals survive the FFN pathway and reach the output distribution with semantic specificity. Qualitative steering effects transfer across architectures from 3B to 31B.

</details>


### [141] [Source-Grounded Data Generation for Text-to-JSON Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20072)
*Sunghee Ahn,Guijin Son,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STAGE是一种基于电子表格锚定的文本到JSON数据生成流水线，通过LLM合成报告与JSON Schema，并利用底层电子表格验证真值，从而生成高质量训练数据，显著提升了小模型的结构化提取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统行业（如金融、医疗）依赖长文本非结构化文档存储高价值信息，将其可靠地提取为结构化JSON是自动化处理的前提。然而，构建可靠且可扩展的文本到JSON训练数据仍面临挑战，现有方法缺乏对生成数据中真值的有效验证。

Method: 提出STAGE（Spreadsheet-grounded Text-to-JSON Artifact GEneration）流水线：以电子表格为源锚定，利用LLM合成报告文本和JSON Schema，同时将生成的ground-truth值与原始电子表格进行交叉验证，确保数据质量。在此基础上构建了包含851个测试样本的源锚定基准STAGE-Eval。

Result: 在STAGE-Eval基准上，使用STAGE生成的训练数据对Qwen3-4B模型进行微调后，精确匹配率从31.37%提升至74.27%，值准确率从45.46%提升至90.69%，显著优于现有数据生成方法。

Conclusion: STAGE通过源锚定验证机制，有效解决了文本到JSON训练数据中的真值可靠性问题，证明了高质量合成数据对小模型结构化信息提取能力的巨大提升作用。

Abstract: From financial filings to clinical records, legacy industries rely heavily on long, unstructured documents to store high-value information. Reliably extracting this information into structured, machine-readable representations is a key prerequisite to making the contents accessible to automated systems. JSON is a natural target for such structured extraction, yet constructing reliable and scalable text-to-JSON training data remains challenging. To address this gap, we propose STAGE (Spreadsheet-grounded Text-to-JSON Artifact GEneration), a source-grounded data generation pipeline that constructs reports and JSON schema by using LLMs for scalable synthesis while validating ground-truth values against the underlying spreadsheet. Evaluations on STAGE-Eval, our source-grounded benchmark with an 851-example test set, show that STAGE produces stronger training data than existing approaches. This improves Qwen3-4B exact match from 31.37% to 74.27% and value accuracy from 45.46% to 90.69%.

</details>


### [142] [IHUBERT: Vector-Based Semantic Deduplication and Domain-Balanced Pretraining for Persian Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20089)
*Arash Ghafouri,Mahdi Firouzmandi,Hossein Saberi,Mohammad Reza Hasani Ahangar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IHUBERT 是一个基于 RoBERTa-base 架构的单语波斯语预训练模型，在 45GB 精心筛选的语料上从头训练，通过多阶段预处理（包括语义去重和分布平衡）及 BPE 分词器优化，在提取式问答等7项 NLU 基准上取得了领先或具有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波斯语预训练语言模型面临两大瓶颈：一是大规模高质量预训练语料稀缺，二是评估多局限于标准分类和 NER 任务，缺乏对阅读理解等更广泛 NLU 任务的系统评测。

Method: 1. 从 Sepahr-Danesh 集合中筛选 45GB（约 7-8B tokens）语料；2. 多阶段预处理：标准化、精确/近似去重、匿名化、基于向量数据库的语义去重以平衡领域和语域分布；3. 使用 BPE 训练 139k 词汇量的分词器；4. 基于 RoBERTa-base（125M 参数）从头预训练；5. 在 NER、情感分析、主题分类、NLI、提取式 QA、关系抽取共 7 项任务上评测。

Result: 提取式 QA 表现最强：PQuAD F1 88.3542、ParsiNLU-RC F1 49.0987，均排名第一；FarsTail NLI 取得最佳 Macro-F1 0.8350；NER（ParsTwiNER F1 0.8308）和主题分类（DigiMag Macro-F1 0.7953）保持竞争力；关系抽取（PERLEX Macro-F1 0.6684）为最大短板。BPE 与 WordPiece 在等词表大小时，BPE 的子词碎片化程度略低。

Conclusion: IHUBERT 通过语义级筛选的大规模预训练和覆盖分类与理解任务的广泛评估，推动了波斯语语言建模的进步，同时验证了 BPE 分词器在波斯语形态学处理上的优势。

Abstract: Persian pretrained language models (PLMs) are still limited by the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality pretraining corpora and by insufficient evaluation beyond standard classification and NER tasks. We present IHUBERT, a monolingual Persian PLM trained from scratch with the RoBERTa-base encoder (125M parameters) on a 45 GB curated subset of the Sepahr-Danesh collection (about 7-8B tokens). To improve corpus quality and reduce redundancy, we employ a multi-stage preprocessing pipeline that includes normalization, exact and near-duplicate removal, anonymization, and vector-database-based semantic deduplication for distribution balancing control across domains and registers. We additionally train a 139k-vocabulary BPE tokenizer on the full pretraining corpus to better capture Persian morphology and orthographic variation. IHUBERT is evaluated on seven Persian NLU benchmarks covering NER, sentiment analysis, topic classification, NLI, extractive question answering, and relation extraction, using task-standard metrics (entity-level F1, Macro-F1, EM/F1). IHUBERT achieves its strongest gains on extractive QA, ranking first on both PQuAD (F1 88.3542) and ParsiNLU-RC (F1 49.0987), and attains the best result on FarsTail (Macro-F1 0.8350). On NER and topic classification, it remains competitive (e.g., 0.8308 F1 on ParsTwiNER; 0.7953 Macro-F1 on DigiMag), while relation extraction remains the main remaining gap (0.6684 Macro-F1 on PERLEX). A controlled tokenizer ablation on the IHUBERT pretraining corpus shows that BPE yields slightly lower subword fragmentation than WordPiece at matched vocabulary size, supporting our tokenization design. Overall, IHUBERT advances Persian language modeling through semantically curated large-scale pretraining and broad evaluation across both classification and comprehension-oriented tasks.

</details>


### [143] [Self-Preference Is Weak or Absent in Verifiable Instruction-Following Revision: A Four-Model Test Under Genuine Authorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20093)
*William Guey,Pierrick Bougault*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究测试了大型语言模型在修订自己写作时是否存在自我偏好偏差（即拒绝对自己草稿的有效修正）。通过IFEval指令遵循任务和确定性验证器，比较模型作为"作者"与"中立审阅者"时对经过验证的正确编辑的接受率，结果未发现显著的自我偏好效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明LLM在评判时存在自我偏好偏差（偏爱自己生成的文本），这引发了一个关键问题：模型在修订自己写作时是否也会拒绝有效的修正？该研究的动机在于，通过确定性的客观验证来测试这种潜在的自我偏好，而非依赖另一个模型进行评判。

Method: 使用IFEval指令遵循任务：模型先生成一篇草稿，由IFEval官方检查器确认该草稿违反了某条约束，并验证候选编辑修复了该问题；随后，模型在同一对话上下文中作为"原作者"或在全新上下文中作为"中立审阅者"决定接受或拒绝该编辑。跨四个中规模模型家族进行85组作者vs中立对比实验。

Result: 未检测到显著的自我偏好：作者拒绝经过验证的正确修正的比率与中立审阅者基本一致（差距-5.1个百分点，95%置信区间[-12.9, +2.7]）。小规模预实验中发现的"自我怀疑"提示效应在大规模实验中未能复现。唯一稳健的定性发现：当作者拒绝经过验证的正确编辑时，97%的陈述理由是"发现缺陷"（flaw-catching），而非偏好驱动。

Conclusion: 在给定样本量下，不能完全排除约13个百分点以下的效应，但整体证据表明，LLM在修订自己草稿时并未表现出对自我生成内容的系统性偏好，这与已有的评判场景中的自我偏好形成对比。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly review and revise text, including their own. A documented self-preference bias (models favoring their own generations when acting as judges) raises the question of whether models also resist valid corrections to their own writing. We test this in a setting where "valid" is decided not by another model but by a deterministic verifier: instruction-following revision on IFEval. A model writes a draft; the official IFEval checker confirms the draft violates a constraint and that a candidate edit fixes it; the model then accepts or rejects that edit either as the genuine in-context author or as a fresh model that sees the draft neutrally. Across four mid-tier model families and 85 author-versus-fresh comparisons, we find no detectable self-preference: authors reject verified-good fixes to their own drafts at essentially the same rate as fresh models judging the same drafts (gap -5.1 pp, 95% CI [-12.9, +2.7]). A self-skepticism hint from a smaller pilot did not replicate at scale. The one robust observation is qualitative: when authors do reject a verified-good fix, 97% of their stated reasons are flaw-catching rather than preference, that is, about the character of rejections, not an elevated rate. Effects smaller than ~13 pp cannot be excluded at this sample size.

</details>


### [144] [HydraHead: From Head-Level Functional Heterogeneity to Specialized Attention Hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20097)
*Zhentao Tan,Wei Chen,Jingyi Shen,Yao Liu,Xu Shen,Yue Wu,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HydraHead 提出在注意力头（head）维度而非传统的层（layer）维度上混合全注意力（FA）与线性注意力（LA），通过可解释性驱动的头选择策略和尺度归一化融合模块，以极低的训练开销实现超越层级别混合方案的长上下文性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源混合注意力模型多采用逐层（layer-wise）混合策略，但全注意力与线性注意力在层级别上难以有效融合，且注意力混合的设计空间尚未被充分探索。作者通过可解释性分析发现，层内不同注意力头呈现功能异质性（functional specialization），这为在头级别进行异构注意力信号融合提供了自然且合理的粒度。

Method: 提出 HydraHead 架构，核心包括：(1) 基于可解释性的头选择策略——识别"检索关键头"（retrieval-critical heads），仅对这些头保留全注意力，其余头替换为线性注意力；(2) 尺度归一化融合模块——解决 FA 与 LA 头输出之间的分布差异。训练上采用三阶段迁移流水线（three-stage transfer pipeline），结合参数复用与知识蒸馏，实现低训练开销的高性能混合模型。

Result: 在统一训练设置下，HydraHead 在长上下文任务上优于其他混合设计，同时保持强大的通用推理能力。以 7:1 的 LA 对 FA 比例，即可匹敌 3:1 层级别混合方案的长上下文性能。仅使用 15B token 训练，在 512K 上下文长度下相较基线提升超 69%，接近同规模且原生支持 256K 上下文的领先模型 Qwen3.5。

Conclusion: 头级别的注意力混合展现出显著的扩展潜力（scaling potential），为高效长上下文模型设计提供了新的方向。HydraHead 以更少的全注意力计算量实现了更强的长上下文性能，且训练成本极低，验证了 head 维度作为混合粒度的优越性。

Abstract: The quadratic complexity of attention poses a critical bottleneck for long-context processing, spurring interest in hybrid attention designs. Most open-source hybrid models adopt a layer-wise strategy. Yet, prior work has noted the inherent difficulty of integrating Linear Attention (LA) with Full Attention (FA), suggesting that the design space of attention hybridization remains underexplored. To probe this space, we conduct interpretability analysis and observe that layers exhibit block-wise functional similarity, while individual heads within the same layer display distinct functional specialization despite sharing input features. This head-level heterogeneity suggests that the head dimension provides a natural and principled granularity for fusing heterogeneous attention signals. Building on this insight, we introduce HydraHead, a novel architecture that hybridizes FA and LA along the head axis. HydraHead features two key innovations: (1) an interpretability-driven selection strategy that identifies retrieval-critical heads and preserves FA only for them, and (2) a scale-normalized fusion module that reconciles the distributional gap between FA and LA head outputs. By leveraging a three-stage transfer pipeline with parameter reuse and distillation, we achieve high-performance hybrid models with minimal training overhead. Under a unified training setup, HydraHead outperforms other hybrid designs in long-context tasks while maintaining strong general reasoning. With interpretability-driven head selection, it matches a 3:1 layer-wise hybrid's long-context performance at a 7:1 LA-to-FA ratio. Crucially, trained on only 15B tokens, HydraHead achieves over 69% improvement over the baseline at 512K context length, approaching Qwen3.5, a leading model of comparable size with a native context length of 256K. This highlights the significant scaling potential of head-level hybridization.

</details>


### [145] [When Does Streaming Tool Use Help? Characterizing Tool-Intent Stabilization in Streaming Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20113)
*Elroy Galbraith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从“工具意图稳定化”这一全新角度量化分析了流式RAG的延迟隐藏潜力。在CRAG基准上，测量了推测性查询何时收敛到正确答案，推导出延迟隐藏上界H，并验证实际收益；发现在600ms延迟、3词/秒输入的现实条件下，73.9%的查询可实现显著延迟隐藏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式RAG通过并行发起工具查询来降低用户感知延迟，但其收益本质上是查询内在的——只有当正确工具查询在用户说完之前即可确定时，推测才有帮助。现有研究仅报告聚合收益，缺乏对这一“工具意图稳定化”属性的系统测量和理解。

Method: 在CRAG基准（1371个验证问题）上：(1) 测量工具意图稳定化的分布（比较BIR/ORACLE-SUFFICIENCY/TOP-1-SETTLING三种grounding策略）；(2) 推导与模型无关的延迟隐藏上界H，作为工具延迟L和输入节奏δ的函数；(3) 在真实流式pipeline上验证实际收益是否达到或超过理论界；(4) 识别预测早/晚稳定化的查询属性（如问题类型）。全部实验在商用CPU上完成，无需模型训练。

Result: 在L=600ms, δ=3词/秒, θ=0.8的现实操作点下：全基准73.9%的查询可实现显著延迟隐藏。在黄金证据逐字存在且BM25可检索的有利子集（21.3%）上，充足性稳定化的φ_suf被界定在[0.26, 0.281]（早稳定）。问题类型产生显著但粗糙的早/晚分异（Kruskal-Wallis p=0.017, ε²=0.04），为学习型推测触发器的成本效益决策提供了直接依据。

Conclusion: 工具意图稳定化是流式RAG性能的核心内在属性。在现实条件下，大多数查询得益于早期稳定化，可实现显著延迟隐藏。问题类型可作为何时触发推测的实用信号，且该方法无需模型训练、可在商品CPU上运行，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Streaming Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Streaming RAG) reduces user-perceived latency by issuing tool queries in parallel with ongoing user input, before the utterance is complete. Reported gains are aggregate, yet the mechanism's benefit is fundamentally query-intrinsic: speculation can only help when the correct tool query becomes determinable before the user stops speaking or typing. We isolate and measure this property -- tool-intent stabilization, the point in the input stream at which a speculative query's retrieval converges to the answer-bearing result. On the CRAG benchmark (1371 validation questions) we (i) measure the distribution of stabilization, (ii) derive a model-agnostic bound H on the portion of tool latency that can be hidden behind the user's remaining input, as a function of tool latency L and input cadence δ, (iii) validate against a working streaming pipeline that realized savings meet or exceed this bound, and (iv) identify which query properties predict early versus late stabilization. The study requires no model training and runs on commodity CPU hardware. We find that at a realistic operating point (L=600ms, δ=3w/s, θ=0.8), 73.9% of queries across the full benchmark admit substantial latency hiding -- a blended figure that mixes sufficiency stabilization on the 21.3% of questions where gold evidence is verbatim-present and BM25-retrievable (95.2% streamable on this favorable slice) with a grounding-free top-1-settling fallback on the remainder. On the favorable slice, φ_suf is bracketed to [0.26, 0.281] by exact and relaxed grounding -- both early. Question type produces a significant but coarse early/late split (Kruskal-Wallis p=0.017, epsilon^2=0.04), directly informing when a learned speculative trigger is worth its cost.

</details>


### [146] [From Texts to Scores: Tracing the Emergence of Essay Quality Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20152)
*Jiaxu Zuo,Mu You,Kaixin Lan,Tao Fang,Yujia Huo,Henghua Shen,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过对8个LLM在三个数据集上的系统分析，发现作文评分信息以线性可解码的形式编码在隐藏表征中，这些表征在层级间渐进涌现、对提示策略鲁棒、可跨题目部分迁移，非线性探针增益有限，并识别出对评分敏感的"作文评分神经元"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM已显著推动自动作文评分(AES)的发展，但其内部评分机制仍缺乏深入理解，需要系统揭示LLM表征中如何编码作文质量信息。

Method: 在ASAP++、CSEE（英语）和ENEM（葡萄牙语）三个数据集上，对8个LLM的隐藏表征进行线性探针、跨提示泛化、降维分析和神经元级别分析，辅以非线性探针对比和目标化干预实验。

Result: 1) 作文质量信息在LLM表征中以线性可访问形式编码，随层级渐进涌现；2) 表征对提示策略变化保持鲁棒；3) 跨作文题目可部分迁移；4) 非线性探针相比线性探针仅提供边际且不一致的提升；5) 识别出"作文评分神经元"，其激活与分数强相关且对目标干预敏感；6) 神经元层级分布随作文长度系统性偏移，更长作文更依赖深层表征。

Conclusion: LLM编码了与作文质量相关的结构化表征，该研究为基于LLM的AES系统的可解释性提供了新见解。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have substantially transformed Automated Essay Scoring (AES), yet the internal mechanisms underlying LLM-based scoring remain poorly understood. In this work, we systematically analyze the hidden representations of eight LLMs across two English essay datasets (ASAP++, CSEE) and one Portuguese dataset (ENEM). Using linear probing, cross-prompt generalization, dimensionality reduction, and neuron-level analyses, we find consistent evidence that essay quality information is encoded in a linearly accessible form within LLM representations. These representations emerge progressively across layers, remain robust across prompting strategies, and partially transfer across essay prompts despite differences in scoring rubrics. In addition, nonlinear probes provide only marginal and inconsistent improvements over linear probes, suggesting that most essay quality information is already linearly decodable. We further identify individual ``essay scoring neurons'' whose activations strongly correlate with essay scores and whose behavior is sensitive to targeted intervention. Moreover, the layer-wise distribution of these neurons systematically shifts with essay length, with longer essays relying more heavily on deeper layers. Overall, our findings provide evidence that LLMs encode structured representations related to essay quality and offer new insights into the interpretability of LLM-based AES systems.

</details>


### [147] [MedRLM: Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence for Long-Context Clinical Reasoning, Sensor-Guided Screening, Evidence-Grounded Decision Support, and Community-to-Tertiary Referral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20164)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedRLM提出一种递归多模态健康智能框架，将患者病历视为外部临床环境进行递归检查、分解、检索、验证和综合，通过协调多个专业Agent和临床证据图记忆，实现从静态问答到可审计、多模态、工作流感知的临床决策支持的跨越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学LLM和RAG系统依赖单步提示或检索，在面对分布在长程EHR、医学影像、传感器流、指南和转诊约束中的分散临床证据时表现脆弱，无法满足真实临床决策对异构、纵向患者信息进行综合推理的需求。

Method: MedRLM框架包含五个核心设计：(1)将患者病例作为外部临床环境进行递归检查、分解、检索、验证和综合；(2)协调多个专业Agent（临床文本、纵向EHR、医学影像、生理传感器信号、指南检索、不确定性审计、转诊规划）；(3)构建临床证据图记忆（Clinical Evidence Graph Memory），连接患者特异性观察与检索证据、标准化定义、传感器衍生生物标志物及转诊标准；(4)传感器引导的递归触发机制，在检测到异常生理或行为模式时激活深层推理；(5)不确定性门控精炼机制，对高风险或低置信度病例支持临床医生复核。

Result: 论文提出了一个使用公开和受认证临床数据集（涵盖EHR、放射学、ECG、ICU时间序列及转诊代理结果）的真实数据评估设计，但未报告具体实验结果。

Conclusion: MedRLM旨在推动医学AI从静态问答向可审计、多模态、工作流感知的临床决策支持演进，通过递归多模态架构和临床证据图记忆实现更稳健的长程临床推理。

Abstract: Real-world clinical decision support requires reasoning over heterogeneous and longitudinal patient information rather than answering isolated medical questions. However, current medical large language models and retrieval-augmented generation systems often rely on single-step prompting or retrieval, which can be fragile when clinical evidence is distributed across long electronic health records, medical images, sensor streams, guidelines, and referral constraints. This paper proposes MedRLM, a Recursive Multimodal Health Intelligence framework for long-context clinical reasoning, sensor-guided screening, and community-to-tertiary referral support. Instead of compressing all patient information into one prompt, MedRLM treats the patient case as an external clinical environment that can be recursively inspected, decomposed, retrieved, verified, and synthesized. The framework coordinates specialized agents for clinical text, longitudinal EHR, medical imaging, physiological sensor signals, guideline retrieval, uncertainty auditing, and referral planning. It further introduces a Clinical Evidence Graph Memory to connect patient-specific observations with retrieved evidence, standardized definitions, sensor-derived biomarkers, and referral criteria. A sensor-guided recursive triggering mechanism activates deeper reasoning when abnormal physiological or behavioral patterns are detected, while uncertainty-gated refinement supports clinician review for high-risk or low-confidence cases. We also outline a real-data evaluation design using public and credentialed clinical datasets spanning EHR, radiology, ECG, ICU time series, and referral-proxy outcomes. MedRLM aims to move medical AI from static question answering toward auditable, multimodal, and workflow-aware clinical decision support.

</details>


### [148] [ReNikud: Audio-Supervised Hebrew Grapheme-to-Phoneme Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20179)
*Maxim Melichov,Yakov Kolani,Morris Alper*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReNikud提出一种用于现代希伯来语字形到音素（G2P）转换的新方法，通过弱音频监督的ASR伪标注和伪元音标注架构，在字符级别预测IPA音素，超越了已有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希伯来语采用辅音音素文字（abjad），元音大多不写，导致G2P存在严重歧义。传统先预测元音附加符号（nikud）再转IPA的方法受限于标注数据稀缺、缺乏词重音信息，且反映的是正式语法规则而非自然口语发音。直接Seq2Seq预测IPA则受制于数据不足且无法利用abjad特有的字符对齐特性。

Method: ReNikud包含两个核心创新：(1) 利用基于音素的自动语音识别（ASR）在数千小时无标注希伯来语音频上进行伪标注，获得反映自然口语规范的音素转录，无需人工标注；(2) 设计伪元音标注架构，在每个字符位置预测IPA音素，强制引入字符级对齐作为归纳偏置。

Result: 在现有希伯来语G2P基准及新构建的面向口语希伯来语的MILIM基准上，ReNikud均超越了此前的最优方法。

Conclusion: ReNikud有效解决了希伯来语G2P的核心难题，将公开发布代码和模型以促进希伯来语TTS及语音技术发展。

Abstract: Grapheme-to-phoneme (G2P) conversion for Modern Hebrew is needed for applications like text-to-speech (TTS), but is challenging due to the language's abjad writing system, which leaves vowels largely unwritten, creating substantial ambiguity. Standard approaches first predict vowel diacritics (nikud) to produce International Phonetic Alphabet (IPA) transcriptions, but this is limited: vocalization data is scarce and laborious to produce, it does not specify features such as lexical stress, and it reflects formal grammatical rules rather than everyday spoken pronunciation. Direct sequence-to-sequence IPA prediction, meanwhile, struggles on limited data and fails to exploit the character-level alignment characteristic of abjads. Our method, ReNikud, overcomes these limitations with two key insights: (1) Weak audio supervision via a phoneme-based automatic speech recognition (ASR) pseudo-labeling pipeline on thousands of hours of unlabeled Hebrew audio, yielding phonemic transcriptions that reflect natural spoken norms without manual annotation. (2) A pseudo-vocalization architecture that predicts IPA phonemes at each character position, enforcing character-level alignment as an inductive bias. Results on existing Hebrew G2P benchmarks and the new targeted MILIM benchmark for spoken Hebrew show that ReNikud surpasses previous state-of-the-art methods. We will release our code and trained models to support further work on Hebrew TTS and speech technologies.

</details>


### [149] [Pitch Spelling Jazz Lead Sheets, Solo Transcriptions, Classical Piano and Monophonic Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20198)
*Augustin Bouquillard,Florent Jacquemard*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合进行音高拼写（pitch spelling）和调号估计的算法，通过两个优化阶段——模态阶段（逐小节估计局部音阶，以最小化临时变音记号）和调性阶段（基于局部音阶估计全局调号和最终音名）——在MIDI输入上生成最优乐谱记谱，并在爵士、古典等多种数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐转录中，从MIDI音高（半音表示）自动推断正确的音名拼写（如F#还是Gb）和调号是一个核心难题。现有方法往往忽视小节边界信息、局部音阶与全局调号的联合优化，且缺乏对爵士乐等非古典乐种的适应性。作者旨在为爵士独奏音频转录构建数字馆藏，服务于音乐分析、教学和文化遗产保护。

Method: 算法分为两阶段优化：（1）模态阶段：以小节为单位，对每小节的音符集合在所有可能的爵士音阶中进行最短路径搜索，选出使临时变音记号数量最少的局部音阶序列；（2）调性阶段：利用各小节的局部音阶估计整个乐曲的全局调号，并在此基础上确定每个音符的最终音名拼写。此外，作者定义了多种常见爵士音阶之间的新距离度量。

Result: 在多个数据集上进行了评估，包括Real Book爵士主旋律谱、爵士独奏与贝斯线转录、传统曲调、钢琴与单声部古典乐谱。算法在音高拼写准确率和调号估计准确率上表现良好，尤其在爵士乐场景中展现了相较于传统方法的优势。

Conclusion: 该算法有效解决了MIDI到乐谱自动记谱中的音高拼写和调号估计问题，尤其适用于爵士乐转录。提出的两阶段联合优化策略和爵士音阶间距离定义，对音乐信息检索、自动记谱和音乐学研究均有实用价值。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for pitch spelling and key estimation. Given an input in MIDI-like format, containing information on note pitches (expressed in semitones relative to the lowest reference note) and bar boundaries, it estimates the appropriate note names, a global Key Signature, and a local scale for each bar. This related information elements are evaluated jointly during two stages of optimisation. During an initial 'modal' stage, a probable scale is proposed for each bar, minimising the number of accidentals to be printed in the printed score with a shortest-path search. Then, during a second stage called 'tonal', these local scales are used to estimate the Key Signature and note names that would result in the best musical notation for the entire piece. We present evaluations conducted on datasets comprising a variety of digital musical scores: jazz lead sheets taken from the Real Book, transcriptions of recordings of jazz soli and bass lines, traditional tunes, as well as classical scores for piano and monophonic instruments. Our procedure was originally designed for use in music transcription, specifically for building digital collections of jazz solos transcribed from audio recordings, for the purposes of music analysis, teaching and the preservation of cultural heritage. This method should also prove useful for other tasks related to the processing of musical notation. Furthermore, to this end, we have defined new distances between various common jazz scales, which may be of some interest to musicological studies.

</details>


### [150] [CzechDocs: A Multiway Parallel Dataset of Formatted Documents for Minority Languages in Czechia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20212)
*Josef Jon,Ondřej Bojar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CzechDocs 是一个多向平行格式文档数据集（HTML、DOCX、PDF），覆盖捷克语及乌克兰语、英语等少数语种，旨在评估保持文档格式的机器翻译系统。作者在验证集上比较了主流格式保持翻译方法，并公开发布了验证集与评测工具，保留测试集用于未来的共享任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器翻译评测大多忽略文档格式信息，而实际应用中（如网页、文档翻译）保持原始格式至关重要。缺乏面向格式保持翻译的多语种、多格式公开基准数据集，因此需要构建 CzechDocs 以填补这一空白。

Method: 构建 CzechDocs 数据集：收集捷克语及多种少数语种的 HTML、DOCX、PDF 格式平行文档；在验证子集上对当前主流的格式保持机器翻译方法进行对比评测；开发并公开发布评测工具包，同时预留测试集用于未来共享任务。

Result: 论文提供了在验证子集上对多种格式保持机器翻译方法的对比结果（摘要中未给出具体数值），并将验证集和评测工具公开发布，测试集留作后续共享任务使用。

Conclusion: CzechDocs 为格式保持的文档级机器翻译提供了首个多语种、多格式的标准化评测基准，有望推动该领域的研究进展，未来共享任务将进一步促进社区发展。

Abstract: We present CzechDocs, a multiway parallel dataset of formatted documents (HTML, DOCX, and PDF) covering Czech and minority languages used in Czechia-primarily Ukrainian and English, with smaller portions of Vietnamese, Russian and other languages. The dataset is designed to support the evaluation of machine translation systems that aim to preserve document formatting during translation. We provide a comparison of the most common approaches to format-preserving machine translation on a validation subset of the dataset. This validation split, together with the evaluation toolkit, is publicly released for further research. A held-out test split will be reserved for a future shared task focused on document-level translation with formatting preservation.

</details>


### [151] [Actionable Activation Directions for Detecting and Mitigating Emergent Misalignment Across Language Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20225)
*Abdul Rafay Syed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对不安全代码进行微调会导致语言模型产生新兴的对齐偏差（misalignment）。研究发现，在模型最后一层，可通过均值差方向以99.6%的准确率线性分离对齐与偏差激活；模型内减去该方向可显著减少不安全代码输出（21-51点），且具有内容特异性；跨架构迁移虽有行为抑制效果，但缺乏特异性，随机和正交方向表现相当。由此揭示出双层特异性结构：模型内方向因果特异且可操作，跨模型方向因果真实但非特异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在不安全代码上微调语言模型所引发的 emergent misalignment 是否对应一个跨架构共享的、可因果操作的激活空间方向，从而为模型审计与对齐修正提供机制性理解。

Method: 在四个指令微调模型家族（Qwen2.5-1.5B、Gemma-2-2B、Llama-3.2-1B、Ministral-3-3B）上进行相同的微调处理。对每个模型最后一层的激活值计算对齐与偏差状态之间的均值差方向（difference-in-means）；通过减去该方向进行因果干预（causal steering）；采用岭回归映射（ridge regression）实现跨架构方向迁移；并以安全代码方向、随机方向、正交方向作为对照。

Result: 模型内：均值差方向在最后一层以99.6%的准确率分离对齐与偏差激活；减去该方向使代码泄露（code spillover）降低21-51点，安全代码对照组确认内容特异性。跨架构：岭回归映射迁移可产生大幅行为抑制（最高46点），但特异性质控失败——随机方向和正交方向表现相当。Gemma和Qwen作为几何"供体"，Llama作为"受体"，形成非对称迁移拓扑。

Conclusion: 存在双层特异性结构：模型内线性方向具有因果特异性，可用于可操作的矫正；跨模型方向虽因果真实但缺乏特异性。这界定了线性跨架构修正的极限，建议采用模型内探测（within-model probing）进行审计。

Abstract: Fine-tuning language models on insecure code induces emergent misalignment with poorly understood internal structure. We investigate whether this misalignment corresponds to a causally actionable activation-space direction shared across architectures. Across four instruction-tuned model families (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-1B, Ministral-3-3B) finetuned identically, a difference-in-means direction achieves 99.6% separation of aligned and misaligned activations at each model's final layer. Causal steering by subtracting this direction reduces code spillover by 21-51 points, while a secure-code control confirms content specificity. Cross-architecture transfer via ridge regression maps yields large behavioral suppression (up to 46 points) but fails specificity controls as random and orthogonal directions perform comparably. We identify a two-tier specificity structure: within-model directions are causally specific and actionable; cross-model directions are causally real but non-specific. An asymmetric transfer topology emerges, with Gemma and Qwen acting as geometric donors and Llama as a receiver. These findings define the limits of linear cross-architecture correction and recommend within-model probing for auditing.

</details>


### [152] [The Register Gap: A Meaning Intelligence Framework for Nigerian Public Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20255)
*Celestine Achi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"意义智能框架"（MIF），一个面向尼日利亚公共话语的九维度标注与评估体系，旨在区分表层情感与真实交际意图。研究发现，大语言模型在尼日利亚话语上的主要失败模式是语境失败而非翻译失败，且通过向模型提供MIF模式可显著提升其在语域识别、隐含子文本检测及策略行动建议等方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有尼日利亚语言的情感分析基准（如NaijaSenti、AfriSenti）将任务简化为三分类（正面/负面/中性），忽视了一个关键现象：同一话语因说话者、受众和情境不同，可能携带截然相反的语用效力。作者认为AI系统在处理尼日利亚话语时的核心失败模式并非翻译失败，而是语境失败（context failure），因此需要一个更精细的框架来捕捉表层情感与真实交际意图之间的差异。

Method: 作者提出"意义智能框架"（MIF），包含九个评分维度：语域、表层情感、真实意图、反讽、编码子文本、风险等级、标注者置信度、说话者情绪及推荐传播行动。基于该框架，构建了一个30条样本的校准数据集，涵盖标准英语、尼日利亚英语、尼日利亚皮钦语及语码混合语域。在零样本和模式知情提示两种条件下评估前沿语言模型Gemini 2.5 Flash，比较其性能差异。

Result: 核心发现为"语域差距"：零样本条件下语域分类准确率仅为33.3%，而当模型在上下文中接收MIF模式后，准确率提升至73.3%（+40个百分点）。综合"意义智能得分"从73.2提升至78.6（+5.4分），其中实际收益最大的维度为语域识别、编码子文本检测（+10分）和策略行动建议（+10.3分）。

Conclusion: 仅靠扩大语言覆盖范围不足以解决低资源语言环境中的AI失败问题；为模型提供结构化的社会语言语境模式（如MIF）能显著弥合表层信号与真实含义之间的差距。作者已公开框架规范、标注指南及30条公开校准集以支持可复现性，并保留一份私有留存语料用于防污染评估。

Abstract: We introduce the Meaning Intelligence Framework (MIF), a nine-dimension annotation and evaluation schema for Nigerian public discourse that separates surface sentiment from true communicative intent. Existing benchmarks for Nigerian languages, including NaijaSenti and AfriSenti, treat sentiment classification as a three-way polarity task (positive, negative, neutral). We argue that the dominant failure mode of AI systems on Nigerian discourse is not translation failure but context failure: the same utterance carries opposite pragmatic force depending on speaker, audience, and situation. The MIF operationalises this insight across nine scored dimensions: register, surface sentiment, true intent, irony, coded subtext, risk tier, annotator confidence, speaker emotion, and recommended communications action. We construct a 30-item calibration dataset spanning Standard English, Nigerian English, Nigerian Pidgin, and code-mixed registers, and evaluate a frontier language model (Gemini 2.5 Flash) under zero-shot and schema-informed prompting conditions. The headline finding is the Register Gap: zero-shot register classification accuracy is 33.3%, rising to 73.3% (+40 points) when the model receives the MIF schema in-context. The composite Meaning Intelligence Score increases by 5.4 points (73.2 to 78.6) under schema-informed prompting, with the largest practical gains in register identification, coded-subtext detection (+10 points), and strategic action recommendation (+10.3 points). We release the framework specification, annotation guidelines, and the 30-item public calibration set to support reproducibility, while retaining a private holdout corpus for contamination-protected evaluation.

</details>


### [153] [PsyScore: A Psychometrically-Aware Framework for Trait-Adaptive Essay Scoring and ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20287)
*Wei Xia,Jin Wu,Haoran Shi,Xiangyu Wang,Chanjin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PsyScore 是一个融合心理测量理论的自动作文评分框架，通过共享的潜在能力表示将诊断性评分与支架式反馈相结合，包含特质自适应神经IRT评分器、ZPD支架反馈生成器和多视角反馈评估策略三个模块，在ASAP++数据集上实现了有竞争力的评分性能和更符合教学需求的反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AES方法将评分与反馈割裂：神经评分模型缺乏可解释性，而基于LLM的反馈对学习者能力水平不敏感。需要一个统一框架来整合诊断评估与教学支架。

Method: 提出PsyScore框架：(1) Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer：将等级分部评分模型(GPCM)嵌入神经网络架构，实现能力精确估计和心理测量可解释性；(2) ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator：基于诊断出的能力参数调节多智能体反馈策略，适配不同能力水平；(3) Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation Strategy：通过成对偏好判断和学生修订模拟来评估反馈质量。

Result: 在ASAP++数据集上，PsyScore取得了有竞争力的评分性能，同时生成了更具教学对齐性的反馈。

Conclusion: PsyScore通过共享潜在能力表示成功统一了评分与反馈两大功能，证明心理测量理论驱动的框架能够同时提升评分精度与反馈的教学有效性。

Abstract: Effective Automated Essay Scoring (AES) are expected to support both reliable assessment and actionable instructional feedback. However, existing approaches often treat scoring and feedback as separate components: neural scoring models provide limited interpretability, while Large Language Model (LLM)-based feedback is typically insensitive to learners proficiency levels. To address this fragmentation, this work proposes PsyScore, a psychometrically-aware framework that integrates diagnostic assessment with instructional scaffolding through a shared latent ability representation. PsyScore comprises three key modules: a Trait-Adaptive Neural IRT Scorer that incorporates the Graded Partial Credit Model (GPCM) into a neural architecture, enabling the precise estimation of student ability while maintaining psychometric interpretability, a ZPD-Scaffolded Feedback Generator, which conditions multi-agent feedback strategies on the diagnosed ability parameter to adapt instructional focus across different proficiency levels, and a Multi-Perspective Feedback Evaluation Strategy that assesses feedback quality via pairwise preference judgements and student revision simulations. Experiments on the ASAP++ dataset demonstrate that PsyScore achieves competitive scoring performance while providing more pedagogically aligned feedback.

</details>


### [154] [CATCH-ME if you RAG: a dataset of Contextually Annotated multi-Turn Counterspeech against Hate and Misinformation Exchanges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20369)
*Helena Bonaldi,Genoveffa Martone,Marco Guerini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个大规模、专家策划的多语言对话数据集，专注于仇恨言论与错误信息交叉领域。该数据集涵盖五种语言、七个边缘群体，对话锚定于经核验的外部知识，并包含文档级和块级跨度标注，可直接用于RAG系统，旨在训练更具说服力、事实依据更充分的对抗性言论模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论与错误信息在线上频繁重叠，但NLP研究多将其孤立处理。现有基于LLM的零样本对抗性言论生成常产生重复、模糊的回应，亟需高质量示例来引导模型。然而，针对二者交叉领域的现有对抗性言论数据集稀缺，且仅限于单轮英语对话，而真实互动往往跨越多轮、多种语言。

Method: 作者构建了首个大规模、专家策划的多语言对抗性言论对话数据集。数据集锚定于经核验的外部知识（如事实核查文章、NGO报告），并标注了文档级和块级的跨度注释，使其可直接应用于检索增强生成（RAG）系统。覆盖五种语言和七个被针对的边缘群体。

Result: 该数据集为训练和评估更具说服力、事实依据更充分的对抗性言论模型提供了新资源，填补了多轮、多语言、仇恨-错误信息交叉领域的空白。

Conclusion: 通过引入这一多语言、多轮对话且事实锚定的数据集，研究者能够开发更有效的对抗性言论生成模型，应对仇恨言论与错误信息交织的复合威胁，并推动RAG在该领域的实际应用。

Abstract: Online hate speech and misinformation frequently overlap, yet NLP research has mainly treated them in isolation. While LLMs represent a scalable solution for assisting humans in the generation of counterspeech for both threats, zero-shot models frequently generate repetitive and vague responses, underscoring the need for high-quality examples to steer model generation. However, existing counterspeech datasets against the overlap of hate and misinformation are scarce and limited to single-turn English dialogues, while real-life interactions span across multiple turns and languages. To bridge this gap, we introduce the first large-scale, expert-curated, multilingual dataset of dialogues tackling the intersection of hate and misinformation. To ensure factual grounding, the dialogues are also anchored in verified external knowledge (i.e., fact-checking articles and NGO reports) and include document- and chunk-level span annotations, making it directly applicable for RAG systems. Covering five languages and targeting hate directed at seven marginalized groups, this novel resource enables the training and evaluation of more persuasive, factually grounded counterspeech models.

</details>


### [155] [Your Mouse and Eyes Secretly Leak Your Preference: LLM Alignment using Implicit Feedback from Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20482)
*Haw-Shiuan Chang,Jeffrey Gomez,Mehul Patwari,Aryan Sajith,Hamed Zamani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IFLLM数据集，通过采集用户的鼠标轨迹和眼动注视等隐式反馈信号，证明隐式反馈能显著提升奖励模型的预测准确率（55%→64%），并在DPO对齐后使LLM的回复质量提升近三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法依赖显式人类反馈标注，成本高昂且忽视了隐式反馈（如鼠标轨迹、眼动）的价值。隐式反馈已被互联网巨头证明是重要的经济护城河，但尚未被系统性地用于LLM对齐。

Method: 构建IFLLM数据集：招募59名Mechanical Turk工作者，收集1336轮多轮对话中用户对LLM回复的鼠标轨迹和摄像头眼动注视点数据。基于这些隐式反馈信号训练奖励模型，并与纯文本奖励模型对比；进一步将隐式反馈奖励模型应用于DPO（Direct Preference Optimization），在8个LLM上评估回复质量提升。

Result: IFLLM数据集揭示了用户注视行为和鼠标轨迹的高度多样性。基于隐式反馈的奖励模型将文本奖励模型的准确率从55%提升至64%；在DPO对齐后，相对回复质量提升几乎翻了三倍。

Conclusion: 隐式用户反馈蕴含丰富的偏好信号，能够有效补充甚至超越显式文本反馈，显著改善LLM的对齐效果，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: To align a Large Language Model (LLM), most existing methods collect explicit human feedback and train a reward model to predict the human preference based on the response text. These existing methods have two key limitations. First, the users rarely provide explicit feedback for LLM responses, which makes the high-quality preference annotation expensive to collect. Second, the methods do not leverage implicit human feedback, which has proven vital to the economic moats of Internet giants. To quantify the value of implicit feedback, we build a new dataset called IFLLM, which collects 1336 multi-turn questions from the 59 Mechanical Turk workers, their mouse trajectories, and eye gazing points to the LLMs' responses from their webcams. IFLLM shows that the users have very diverse types of gazing behavior and mouse trajectories. Our reward model based on the implicit user feedback boosts the accuracy of the text-based reward model from 55% to 64% and nearly triples the relative response quality improvements after applying the DPO to eight LLMs, demonstrating the value of implicit feedback in the wild. Our data collection website, dataset, and codes can be found at https://github.com/themehulpatwari/llm-implicit-feedback/.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Global Replanning: Hierarchical Recovery for Cross-Device Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20487)
*Shu Yao,Yuhua Luo,Qian Long,Jingru Fan,Zhuoyuan Yu,Yuheng Wang,Lin Wu,Yufan Dang,Huatao Li,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: H-RePlan 是一种面向多设备智能体的分层重规划框架，通过将设备本地策略恢复与全局重规划解耦，并结合统一的 API-CLI-GUI 执行能力，显著提升了跨设备工作流在运行时故障下的鲁棒性。论文同时提出了故障注入基准 HeraBench 用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多设备智能体系统在任务分解与跨设备分配方面较为成熟，但故障恢复粒度粗糙——通常仅支持重试相同策略、重新分配子任务或修订全局计划，缺乏对设备本地策略空间的系统建模，无法区分可在当前设备内修复的故障与需要跨设备重规划的故障。

Method: 提出 H-RePlan 分层重规划框架：(1) 为每个设备配备可互换的执行策略（API、CLI、GUI），构建设备本地策略空间；(2) 设计紧凑的跨层故障抽象，将设备本地策略恢复与编排器层面的全局重规划分离；(3) 当执行失败时，先在设备本地策略空间中尝试修复，失败后再上报至全局编排器进行跨设备重规划。同时构建 HeraBench 基准，在 Linux 和 Android 设备上构建跨设备工作流并注入策略级和设备级故障。

Result: 在 HeraBench 上，H-RePlan 在完成率、指令遵循度和完美通过率方面均显著优于单策略基线及粗粒度多设备基线，同时在实现可靠端到端成功的前提下降低了 token 消耗。

Conclusion: 范围感知的分层恢复机制对于鲁棒的多设备智能体执行至关重要。H-RePlan 通过分离设备本地恢复与全局重规划，有效提升了跨设备工作流的容错能力和执行效率。

Abstract: Real-world computer-use tasks often span multiple applications and devices, requiring agents to coordinate heterogeneous environments under dynamic runtime failures. Existing multi-device agent systems support task decomposition and cross-device assignment, but recovery remains largely coarse-grained: when execution fails, they typically retry the same strategy, reassign the subtask, or revise the global plan, without systematically modeling the device-local strategy space. This limits their ability to distinguish failures that can be repaired within the current device from those that require cross-device replanning. We propose \textbf{H-RePlan}, a hierarchical replanning framework for multi-device agents with unified API--CLI--GUI execution. H-RePlan equips each device with interchangeable execution strategies and separates device-local strategy recovery from orchestrator-level global replanning through a compact cross-layer failure abstraction. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{HeraBench}, a fault-injected benchmark that constructs cross-device workflows over Linux and Android devices and injects strategy- and device-level failures. Experiments show that H-RePlan substantially outperforms single-strategy and coarse-grained multi-device baselines, achieving higher completion, instruction adherence, and perfect-pass rates while reducing the token cost required for reliable end-to-end success. These results demonstrate that scope-aware hierarchical recovery is essential for robust multi-device agent execution.

</details>


### [157] [StylisticBias: A Few Human Visual Cues Drive Most Social Biases in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20527)
*Shaghayegh Kolli,Timo Cavelius,Nafiseh Nikeghbal,Samantha Dalal,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了StylisticBias基准，通过控制身份固定、单一属性变化的方式，系统评估MLLM中由视觉线索驱动的社会偏见，发现年龄、体型和时尚风格等少数属性主导了模型判断的偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在敏感场景中广泛应用，但影响其社会判断的视觉线索尚不清楚。先前研究往往比较不同个体，难以区分外表效应与身份差异，因此需要一个能隔离单一视觉属性影响的受控评估框架。

Method: 构建StylisticBias基准：生成500张真实感基础面孔，对每张面孔施加约50种单属性变化（如年龄、体型、发型、妆容、时尚风格等），生成约25K张图像。保持身份固定，每次仅改变一个视觉属性，在25个二元社会判断场景中评估六款MLLM，测量各属性对判断偏移的贡献。

Result: 年龄和体型在身份层面效应中占主导地位；时尚风格等视觉线索驱动最大的属性层面偏移。约15个属性解释了近80%的总变异，偏见高度集中。在语义与外表对齐的判断（尤其是社会经济和风格相关判断）中，模型敏感度最强。

Conclusion: MLLM的社会偏见集中于少数视觉属性，StylisticBias为细粒度偏见评估提供了有效工具，并已开源代码与数据集。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in personally and societally consequential settings, yet the visual cues that shape how these models judge people remain poorly understood. Prior work often compares different (groups of) individuals, making it difficult to separate appearance effects from identity differences. We introduce StylisticBias, a controlled benchmark for evaluating attribute-level social bias in MLLMs. We generate 500 photorealistic base faces and create about 50 single-attribute variations per face, producing about 25K images. This design keeps identity fixed and changes one visual attribute at a time. It lets us measure how specific cues shift model judgments. We evaluate six MLLMs across 25 binary social judgment scenarios. We find that age and body type dominate identity-level effects, while fashion style and other visual cues drive the largest attribute-level shifts. We further find that about 15 attributes account for nearly 80\% of the total variation, showing that bias is concentrated in a small set of visual cues. Sensitivity is strongest in judgments that are semantically aligned with appearance, especially socioeconomic and style-related judgments. We release StylisticBias as a benchmark for fine-grained bias evaluation in multimodal models. Code and dataset: https://github.com/timo-cavelius/StylisticBias and https://hf.co/datasets/shaghayegh/stylistic-bias-dataset.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [Overfitted high-dimensional matrix factorizations via adaptive spectral shrinkage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19540)
*Lorenzo Mauri,David B. Dunson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: EigenBayes 是一种快速贝叶斯因子模型方法，利用谱估计和自适应经验贝叶斯校准，实现解析后验分布，避免MCMC，在近似因子模型和低信噪比场景下能有效选择潜在维度并收缩冗余成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据因子模型中潜在维度 k 的选择极具挑战，尤其当因子模型仅近似成立且处于低信噪比场景时。现有的贝叶斯过拟合因子模型虽然有效，但依赖 MCMC 导致计算代价高昂。需要一种既能自动选择维度、提供不确定性量化，又具备计算高效性的新方法。

Method: 提出 EigenBayes 方法：(1) 对潜在因子进行谱估计；(2) 采用自适应经验贝叶斯校准关键超参数；(3) 所得后验分布在结果间可分解且解析可处理，从而绕过了 MCMC；(4) 利用结构化收缩先验自动将冗余潜在成分收缩至零，并自适应各结果的信噪比和潜在维度。

Result: EigenBayes 在理论上建立了良好的渐近性质；在数值实验和基因组学应用中，其经验表现优于当前最先进的替代方法，同时大幅提升了计算速度，并提供有效的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: EigenBayes 成功将贝叶斯因子模型的计算效率与自适应维度选择、不确定性量化相结合，在保持统计优良性的同时显著降低计算负担，为高维近似因子模型分析提供了实用且强大的新工具。

Abstract: Factor models are popular approaches for analyzing high-dimensional data to extract low-rank signals and estimate covariances. They decompose the covariance matrix as the sum of low-rank and diagonal components. A key issue is how to choose the latent dimension $k$, which is particularly challenging when the factor model only holds approximately and in low signal-to-noise scenarios. Bayesian overfitted factor models specify an upper bound on $k$ and rely on structured shrinkage priors to effectively remove extra components. Such approaches are popular and effective, but computationally expensive. We propose a much faster \texttt{EigenBayes} approach that provides valid uncertainty quantification, based on spectral estimation of latent factors and adaptive empirical Bayes calibration of key hyperparameters. The resulting posterior distribution factorizes across outcomes and is analytically tractable, bypassing Markov chain Monte Carlo. We show that \texttt{EigenBayes} adapts to the signal-to-noise ratio of each outcome and latent dimension, while shrinking superfluous latent components to zero. We establish favorable asymptotic properties and demonstrate strong empirical performance in numerical experiments and a genomics application, where EigenBayes outperforms state-of-the-art alternatives.

</details>


### [159] [SCOPE Shrinkage: A Unified Framework for Wavelet Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19572)
*Dixon Vimalajeewa,Vijini Lakmini,Malith Premarathna,Fabrizio Ruggeri,Brani Vidakovic*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE收缩框架——基于对称单峰分布的累积分布函数构造的符号保持收缩规则族，通过双参数分离尺度与形状效应，具有奇性、单调性、连续性、收缩性等结构性质，并兼具贝叶斯MAP估计与惩罚似然解释，在仿真中表现与经典小波去噪方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小波去噪中，传统收缩方法（如硬/软阈值）在近零处的强收缩与尾部无偏性之间存在权衡，缺乏一个统一、灵活且具有概率解释的框架来系统地生成多种收缩剖面。作者希望利用对称分布的CDF作为自然设计原则，构建一个既可解释又在参数选择上灵活可调的收缩规则族。

Method: 以对称单峰分布的标准化累积分布函数为核心构件，提出SCOPE收缩规则的一般形式：通过位置参数和尺度参数分别控制阈值位置和过渡锐度。理论分析在正则条件下建立了规则的奇性、单调性、连续性、收缩性及混合表示（连接软阈值算子）。进一步给出贝叶斯解释：SCOPE规则可表示为适当对称单峰先验下的精确MAP估计，并对应偶惩罚函数。数据驱动参数选择采用Stein无偏风险估计（SURE）。仿真基于Donoho-Johnstone标准测试函数，与多种经典小波去噪方法对比。

Result: 理论层面，SCOPE规则被证明具有符号保持、奇函数、单调非降、Lipschitz连续且为收缩映射等性质，并可通过混合表示与软阈值算子相关联。贝叶斯层面，规则族对应一类非降的偶惩罚函数，特定子类（如Logistic、均匀、Cauchy分布对应的规则）是精确的MAP估计。仿真表明，SCOPE在标准测试函数上的去噪性能与多种成熟方法竞争性相当，同时保持高度可解释性和结构灵活性。

Conclusion: 以对称分布的CDF为设计核心构建收缩规则是一条自然且通用的路径。SCOPE框架将概率形状（如Logistic、均匀、Cauchy）直接映射为收缩行为，实现了近零强收缩与尾部渐近无偏之间的平滑插值，双参数化设计使阈值定位与过渡锐度可独立调节，兼具频率学派的SURE自适应选择与贝叶斯学派的MAP估计解释，为小波去噪及相关估计问题提供了一种统一、灵活且理论完备的收缩工具。

Abstract: We introduce Symmetric CDF Oriented Probability Enhanced (SCOPE) shrinkage, a unified family of sign-preserving shrinkage rules constructed from centered cumulative distribution functions of symmetric unimodal distributions. The proposed framework generates a broad class of attenuation profiles that interpolate between strong local shrinkage near zero and asymptotically unbiased behavior in the tails. A general formulation is developed that separates scale and shape effects through two interpretable parameters, allowing effective threshold location and transition sharpness to be controlled independently. Under explicit regularity assumptions, structural properties of SCOPE shrinkage are established, including oddness, monotonicity, continuity, contractivity, and a mixture representation that connects the rules to softened thresholding operators. A Bayesian and penalized likelihood interpretation is also developed: SCOPE rules admit even penalty representations that are nondecreasing in coefficient magnitude, and suitable subclasses arise as exact maximum a posteriori estimators under proper symmetric unimodal priors. Representative examples based on logistic, uniform, and Cauchy distributions illustrate how probabilistic shape governs shrinkage behavior. Data driven parameter selection for smooth subclasses is discussed via Stein-type unbiased risk estimation. Oracle calibrated simulation studies on standard Donoho-Johnstone test functions show that SCOPE shrinkage performs competitively with several established wavelet denoising methods, while retaining a high degree of interpretability and structural flexibility. The results highlight centered distribution functions as a natural and versatile design principle for shrinkage in wavelet denoising and related estimation problems.

</details>


### [160] [Machine Learning Integrated in Wavelet Shrinkage (MLShrink)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19580)
*Dixon Vimalajeewa,Vijini Lakmini,Brani Vidakovic*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: MLShrink 是一种双阈值小波去噪方法，将传统小波收缩与机器学习相结合：低于下阈值的系数被丢弃，高于上阈值的系数保留，中间过渡带的系数则利用局部小波域特征进行分类判断，从而在保持经典方法简洁性的同时，实现对模糊系数的自适应决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硬/软阈值方法仅依据系数幅度决定保留与否，对于处于信号与噪声难以区分的中间区域的系数过于僵化，亟需一种能够在此模糊地带做出数据自适应决策的去噪方法。

Method: 提出 MLShrink 双阈值架构：设置下阈值和上阈值，将小波系数分为三个区域——低于下阈值的直接归零，高于上阈值的直接保留，中间带系数则利用局部小波域特征训练分类器进行判别。同时构建了适配该架构的理论框架，包括非扩张支持选择规则、基于 oracle 的风险分解，以及分类器性能假设下的 oracle 一致性结果。

Result: 理论分析表明，MLShrink 的 excess risk 由中间带分类误差决定，并在适当分类器条件下具有 oracle 一致性。在标准基准信号上的仿真实验显示，MLShrink 与多种已有小波收缩方法相比具有竞争力，尤其在不规则、边缘丰富或非平滑结构信号上表现突出。

Conclusion: MLShrink 通过在中间阈值带上引入机器学习分类决策，建立了经典小波去噪与现代统计学习之间有效且可解释的桥梁。

Abstract: Data encountered in practice are frequently contaminated by additive noise, and wavelet shrinkage remains a fundamental tool for recovering underlying signals in nonparametric estimation. Classical procedures such as hard and soft thresholding decide whether to retain a wavelet coefficient almost entirely from its magnitude. Although effective in many settings, these rules can be too rigid for coefficients whose magnitudes fall in an intermediate region where the distinction between signal and noise is uncertain. We propose MLShrink, a two-threshold wavelet denoising procedure that combines wavelet shrinkage with machine learning. Coefficients below a lower threshold are discarded, coefficients above an upper threshold are retained, and coefficients in the intermediate band are classified using local wavelet-domain features. In this way, MLShrink preserves the simplicity of classical thresholding away from the decision boundary while allowing data-adaptive decisions for ambiguous coefficients. The paper also develops a theoretical framework tailored to this architecture. We show that MLShrink is a nonexpansive support-selection rule, derive an oracle-based risk decomposition showing that excess denoising risk is determined by classification errors on the undecided band, and establish an oracle-consistency result under suitable assumptions on classifier performance. Simulation experiments on standard benchmark signals indicate that MLShrink is competitive with several established wavelet shrinkage methods and is especially effective for signals with irregular, edge-rich, or non-smooth structure. These findings suggest that learned decisions on the intermediate threshold band provide a useful and interpretable connection between classical wavelet denoising and modern statistical learning.

</details>


### [161] [Calibration without labels in multiple testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19737)
*Adway S. Wadekar,Jake A. Soloff*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究多重假设检验中如何将局部错误发现率（local FDR）解释为近似校准的概率预测。作者通过构造基于有序p值间隔（spacings）的伪标签，使得局部错误发现率成为其回归目标，从而绕过了多重检验中真值永远不可观测的困境。该方法解锁了事后校准工具在多重检验中的应用，并在大规模实证调查中发现，广泛使用的q值可能存在严重校准偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多重假设检验中，经验贝叶斯方法可对单个假设提供概率性声明（如局部错误发现率），但这些声明在模型误设下的可解释性存疑。与概率预测不同，多重检验中的真实标签永远无法观测，因此校准必须通过随机方式间接建立。现有方法缺乏对这类概率声明进行校准评估的有效手段。

Method: 作者借鉴概率预测的校准概念，核心创新在于构造一组"伪标签"：利用有序p值的间隔（spacings）分布特性，推导出局部错误发现率作为这些伪标签的回归目标函数。基于此构造，现有的事后校准（post-hoc calibration）评估与校正工具可直接应用于多重检验场景。

Result: 该方法成功将概率预测中的校准框架迁移到多重检验领域。在大规模实证调查（涵盖已发表的心理学和神经科学文献）中，作者发现基于错误发现率（FDR）的常用误差度量——q值，可能存在严重的校准偏差（miscalibration），即其提供的概率声明并不可靠。

Conclusion: 通过伪标签构造，局部错误发现率可被理解为近似校准的零假设预测，即使在模型误设下也能提供可解释的错误概率。该框架揭示q值等常用指标可能存在严重校准问题，对科研实践中依赖q值进行发现声明的可靠性提出了警示，并为多重检验领域引入了成熟的事后校准工具。

Abstract: Large-scale hypothesis testing supports probability claims about individual hypotheses, as in empirical Bayes methods for estimating local false discovery rates. We study how such claims can be interpreted as approximately calibrated forecasts of the null hypothesis, yielding interpretable error probabilities even under model misspecification. Our approach draws conceptual inspiration from probabilistic forecasting but addresses a different challenge: unlike forecasting, where labels are eventually observed, in multiple testing the ground truth is never revealed, so calibration must be assessed stochastically and established indirectly. We address this challenge by constructing a set of pseudo-labels, derived from the spacings of ordered $p$-values, which have the local false discovery rate as their regression target. Our construction unlocks existing tools for assessing and performing post-hoc calibration in multiple testing. Notably, we find on a large-scale empirical survey of published psychology and neuroscience literature that the $q$-value, a popular error measure based on the false discovery rate, can be severely miscalibrated.

</details>


### [162] [A Bayesian spatio-temporal nearest neighbor Gaussian process model for pooled genetic data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19743)
*Imke Botha,Tianxiao Hao,Lucinda E. Harrison,Nick Golding,Daniel J. Weiss,Jennifer A. Flegg*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用最近邻高斯过程（NNGP）模型从汇总等位基因计数数据中推断单倍型频率的方法，并开发了新型序列蒙特卡洛平方（SMC²）算法进行参数推断，在非洲抗疟药耐药性遗传数据上验证了该方法对3个和6个遗传标记的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模遗传数据集通常仅汇总不同遗传标记的总等位基因计数。从此类合并数据推断单倍型频率（即多位点等位基因组合的频率）是一项挑战。此前该领域的时空建模因计算成本高而被限制在最多3个标记，急需一种能随标记数和观测数良好扩展的方法。

Method: 作者提出使用最近邻高斯过程（NNGP）模型，以改善模型随标记数和观测数增加时的扩展能力。为推断模型参数，开发了一种新型序列蒙特卡洛平方算法（SMC²），该算法利用带祖先采样的粒子吉布斯方法对NNGP函数值进行变异。该方法在观测数量和NNGP数量上均具有线性计算成本，并可广泛应用于各类NNGP模型。

Result: 以非洲抗疟药耐药性相关遗传数据为案例研究，在3个和6个遗传标记的数据集上实证展示了该方法的扩展性结果，证明其能突破此前仅限于3个标记的计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: 所提出的NNGP模型结合SMC²算法，成功解决了从汇总等位基因计数数据中推断单倍型频率的可扩展性问题，将时空建模能力从3个标记扩展至6个及以上标记，并具有向更广泛NNGP模型推广的潜力。

Abstract: Large scale genetic datasets often aggregate the total allele counts of distinct genetic markers. Inferring haplotype frequencies (i.e.\ the frequency of multimarker alleles) from these pooled data is a challenge. Previous spatio-temporal modelling in this context has been limited to 3 markers due to the computational cost. In this work, we propose a nearest neighbor Gaussian process (NNGP) model to improve scaling with the number of markers and observations. To infer the parameters of our model, we develop a novel sequential Monte Carlo squared algorithm, which uses particle Gibbs with ancestor sampling to mutate the NNGP function values. The latter has a linear cost in the number of observations and the number of NNGPs, and can be applied to a broad range of NNGP models. As a case study, we analyse genetic data relating to antimalarial drug resistance in Africa, and show our scaling results empirically on a 3 and 6 genetic marker dataset.

</details>


### [163] [The Ghosh-Lin and Fine-Gray models for a mix of administrative and random censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19892)
*Thomas H. Scheike,Christian Mirian,Isao Yokota,Giuliana Cortese*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文讨论了如何在同时存在行政删失和随机删失的情况下，对复发事件（Ghosh-Lin模型）和竞争风险（Fine-Gray模型）这两种边际回归模型进行正确估计，通过结合两种删失类型的处理方式，在最少建模假设下获得一致估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际的大型中心注册库或临床试验中，所有受试者的行政删失时间是已知的，但通常还会有一部分受试者发生随机删失。现有的边际回归模型（如Ghosh-Lin模型和Fine-Gray模型）对纯行政删失可通过修改风险集简单处理，对纯随机删失则需要逆概率删失加权（IPCW）。然而，当两种删失同时存在时，尚无明确的方法来统一处理这种混合删失情形。

Method: 作者针对两种常用的回归方法——带终末事件的复发事件Ghosh-Lin模型和竞争事件Fine-Gray模型，提出了一种组合策略：对于已知的行政删失，通过修改风险集来处理；对于随机删失部分，则采用逆概率删失加权（IPCW）。该方法在最少建模假设下，将两种不同类型的删失处理方式融合在一起，以获得参数的正确估计。

Result: 论文展示了在同时存在行政删失和随机删失的混合删失情形下，如何通过结合风险集修改和IPCW两种技术，实现对Ghosh-Lin模型和Fine-Gray模型参数的一致估计，且该方法仅需最少的建模假设。

Conclusion: 在行政删失和随机删失并存的常见实践场景中，可以通过将针对不同删失类型的调整策略进行组合，从而在边际回归模型框架下获得正确的参数估计，这为生物医学领域中的复发事件分析和竞争风险分析提供了更为实用的估计方法。

Abstract: Recurrent events or competing risks regression models are often applied in the bio-medical setting and both can be considered as marginal models. In presence of right-censoring, such models need to be adjusted to give consistent estimators. When censoring is administrative, marginal regression models are particularly easy to estimate. However, when censoring is instead acting randomly, inverse probability of censoring weighting (IPCW) adjustments are typically considered to obtain parameter estimates. This technique relies on a censoring-weights adjustment via a correct censoring model, but for administrative censoring the adjustment is done correctly simply by modifying the risk-set. In practice for large central registries or some clinical trials, the administrative censoring time will be known for all subjects, but there will typically also be a proportion of subjects that are censored at random. In this work, we consider two frequently used regression approaches, the Ghosh-Lin model for recurrent events with terminal events and the Fine-Gray model for competing events. For these two settings, when both administrative and random censoring are present, we demonstrate how to obtain correct estimation by dealing with the combination of the two different types of censoring relying on a minimum of modeling assumptions.

</details>


### [164] [Built-in Selection Bias in Proportional Hazards Models with Omitted Covariates: Simulation Evidence and Alternative Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19982)
*Ayoub Bifenzi,Helene Jacqmin-Gadda*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Cox比例风险模型中的风险比（HR）在存在未测量异质性时的非可坍缩性问题。即使是在随机对照试验中，忽略影响结局的重要协变量也会导致HR估计产生内在选择偏倚。作者通过模拟评估了偏倚程度，并比较了脆弱模型、加速失效时间模型、Kaplan-Meier曲线等替代方法的稳健性，最后以RTOG 9202试验数据进行实证说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在时间-事件分析中，Cox比例风险模型估计的风险比（HR）是最常用的治疗效果度量，但HR具有非可坍缩性——因其在每个时间点都以生存为条件。即使是在随机对照试验中，当存在被遗漏的重要预后协变量（即使与治疗分配独立）时，HR也会因未测量的异质性而产生固有的选择偏倚。这一问题在应用研究中常被忽视，因此需要系统梳理其机制、评估偏倚程度，并比较各种稳健替代方法的性能。

Method: 作者采用模拟研究方法：首先在不同未测量异质性情景下，评估半参数Cox PH模型和参数PH模型中HR估计的偏倚程度；然后将这些标准模型与四种替代方法进行比较——（1）脆弱模型估计的HR、（2）加速失效时间模型的回归参数、（3）基于Kaplan-Meier曲线的非参数生存差异估计、（4）基于含时间依赖效应的Cox模型的生存差异估计。最后以RTOG 9202随机对照试验数据进行实证应用。

Result: 模拟结果表明，当存在未测量的预后异质性时，标准Cox PH模型估计的风险比会向零偏倚（即趋向于1），且偏倚程度随着异质性方差增大而加剧。脆弱模型能够在一定程度上纠正这种偏倚，但其依赖于对脆弱项分布的正确设定。加速失效时间模型的回归参数和基于Kaplan-Meier曲线或时间依赖效应Cox模型的生存差异估计，则对未测量异质性表现出更好的稳健性。RTOG 9202实际数据分析进一步验证了这些替代方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 在存在未测量异质性的情况下，标准Cox PH模型的风险比估计存在内在偏倚，即使在随机对照试验中也不例外。研究者应当意识到这一局限性，并在分析中考虑使用脆弱模型、AFT模型、基于生存曲线的非参数估计或允许时间依赖效应的Cox模型等替代方法，以获得更可靠的治疗效果评估。

Abstract: In time-to-event analysis, the hazard ratio (HR) derived from the Cox proportional hazards (PH) model is the most commonly used and widely reported measure for assessing treatment effects. However, hazard ratios are non-collapsible due to their inherent conditioning on survival up to each time point. As a result, they are subject to built-in selection bias in the presence of unmeasured heterogeneity arising from omitted important covariates, even when these covariates are independent of the main exposure at baseline, as is the case in randomized controlled trials. This article aims to provide an overview of key findings from the literature on how unobserved heterogeneity, due to omitted covariates that affect the outcome, can bias the estimation of the treatment hazard ratio in standard proportional hazards models, even in randomized trials where treatment is assigned independently of such covariates. Through simulations, we evaluate the extent of bias in the semi-parametric Cox PH model and parametric PH model under various scenarios of unmeasured heterogeneity. We then compare these standard models to alternative approaches that either account for this issue or are considered robust to it. These alternatives include the hazard ratio estimated from frailty models, regression parameters from an Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model, and survival differences between treatment groups estimated nonparametrically using Kaplan-Meier curves or based on a Cox model with time-dependent effect of the exposure. We illustrate the practical relevance of the explored alternatives through a real data application to a randomized controlled trial from the Radiation Therapy Oncology Group (RTOG 9202).

</details>


### [165] [A minimum-risk and cost-efficient two-sample sequential testing framework for the shifted exponential models with application to precipitation data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20069)
*Ashwani Rajput,Neeraj Joshi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对两个移位指数分布位置参数比较问题，提出一种双重序贯抽样框架下的假设检验方法，能在控制第一类错误的同时最小化包含第二类错误和抽样成本的损失函数，并证明其具备一阶、二阶效率及二阶风险效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在尺度参数未知且不等的条件下，最优固定样本量依赖于未知尺度参数，导致固定样本设计无法在实践中达到期望的检验精度。需要一种能够克服这一困难的序贯抽样方法。

Method: 提出双重序贯抽样假设检验程序：通过控制第一类错误概率在预定水平，同时最小化一个综合考虑第二类错误概率与抽样成本的损失函数。推导最优固定样本量表达式，并在此基础上构建双重序贯抽样方案，以适应尺度参数未知且不等的情形。

Result: 理论证明所提方法具有一阶效率、二阶效率和二阶风险效率等优良渐近性质。模拟研究和基于气象站强降水事件的实证分析验证了方法的实际有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: 双重序贯抽样程序有效解决了移位指数分布下尺度参数未知不等时的位置参数比较问题，兼顾统计精度与抽样成本，具有良好的理论和实用价值。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of comparing the location parameters of two shifted exponential models through a novel double sequential sampling framework. The proposed hypothesis testing procedure is developed by controlling the type I error probability at a preassigned level while minimizing a loss function that incorporates both the type II error probability and the associated sampling cost. The corresponding optimal fixed-sample-size expressions are shown to depend on unknown scale parameters, rendering the desired testing accuracies unattainable in practice under fixed-sample designs. To overcome this difficulty, a double sequential sampling procedure is proposed to test the difference between location parameters when the scale parameters are unknown and unequal. The proposed methodology is shown to possess desirable asymptotic properties, including first-order efficiency, second-order efficiency, and second-order risk efficiency. Extensive simulation studies and a real-data application that involves heavy precipitation episodes at meteorological stations demonstrate the practical effectiveness and applicability of the proposed procedure.

</details>


### [166] [Community detection in small-sample ordinal regimes: A benchmarking framework for Delphi data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20114)
*Yuri Calleo,Simone Di Zio,Fabrizio Maturo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对德尔菲法中高维问卷项目与有限专家样本导致的秩亏问题，本文提出从变量中心协方差模型转向网络中心连通性模型，利用社区发现算法在图拓扑上识别潜在主题结构，将专家判断的共线性重新解读为凝聚力的拓扑信号，为小样本条件下的降维提供了稳健的自动化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德尔菲法共识数据的统计建模面临一个关键瓶颈：问卷项目维度高而专家样本量小，这种秩亏（rank deficiency）导致传统潜变量模型（如PCA）结构不稳定且易过拟合。现有方法将专家判断间的共线性视为需正则化的统计冗余，缺乏在小样本条件下保持结构稳定性和心理测量一致性的有效降维手段。

Method: 提出从"变量中心协方差模型"向"网络中心连通性模型"的范式转换。具体而言，将项目间相关性映射为加权图拓扑，采用基于模拟的基准测试框架，利用社区发现算法（community detection）识别潜在主题结构。系统评估了基于结构密度、信息流和谱划分的拓扑方法在合成数据集上的稳健性，合成数据模拟了德尔菲共识数据的典型病理条件（包括序数量表和系统性噪声）。

Result: 在高维低样本量（HDLSS）条件下，基于网络拓扑的方法有效解决了谱不稳定性和秩亏问题。社区发现算法能够稳定地识别潜在主题结构，而传统因子分析在此类小样本条件下失效。专家判断的共线性被成功重新解读为一种凝聚力的拓扑信号，而非需要消除的统计冗余。

Conclusion: 将共线性从统计冗余重构为拓扑凝聚力信号，是本文的核心方法论贡献。所提框架为研究者提供了一种结构化、自动化的降维程序，在标准因子分析失效的小样本条件下仍能确保结构稳定性和心理测量一致性。

Abstract: The statistical modeling of consensus in Delphi data faces a critical bottleneck: the high dimensionality of questionnaire items relative to the limited sample size of expert panels. This rank deficiency leads traditional latent variable models, such as Principal Component Analysis, to be structurally unstable and prone to overfitting. Addressing this methodological gap, this study proposes a transition from variable-centric covariance models to network-centric connectivity models. By mapping item correlations onto a weighted graph topology, we present a simulation-based benchmark that utilizes community detection algorithms to identify latent thematic structures, effectively addressing the spectral instability and rank deficiency typical of high-dimensional, low-sample-size regimes. The research systematically evaluates the robustness of topological approaches based on structural density, information flow, and spectral partitioning against synthetic datasets designed to replicate the pathological conditions of consensus data, including ordinal scales and systemic noise. The central methodological contribution lies in demonstrating that collinearity among expert judgments - traditionally treated as statistical redundancy to be regularized - can be effectively reinterpreted as a topological signal of cohesion. This framework provides researchers with a structured and automated procedure for dimensionality reduction, ensuring structural stability and psychometric consistency even in small-sample regimes where standard factor analysis breaks down.

</details>


### [167] [A case study of causal mediation using Bayesian nonparametrics and semiparametric corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20148)
*Yuhua Zhang,Michael J. Daniels*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出截断富集狄利克雷过程混合模型（EDPM），结合Metropolis-Hasting聚类重分配算法和基于高效影响函数的一步后验校正，在存在处理后混杂因素的中介分析中估计自然直接效应（NDE）和自然间接效应（NIE），实现了贝叶斯非参数模型下特定因果估计量的可靠推断及优良的频率学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果中介分析中常存在处理后混杂因素（post-treatment confounders），传统方法难以处理。贝叶斯非参数模型虽能灵活建模复杂联合分布，但从联合分布中提取特定因果估计量（NDE/NIE）时，其后验分布往往缺乏良好的频率学性质（如正确覆盖），这是一个亟待解决的关键问题。

Method: 采用截断EDPM模型进行贝叶斯非参数建模；引入高效的聚类重分配Metropolis-Hasting算法改善分块Gibbs抽样的混合性能；基于高效影响函数（EIF）实施一步后验校正，使特定因果估计量的后验具备优良频率学性质。

Result: 通过模拟研究验证了方法的性能，并将该方法应用于一项体重管理临床试验，评估其因果中介效应。

Conclusion: 所提出的截断EDPM模型结合EIF后验校正方法，有效解决了贝叶斯非参数框架下特定因果中介效应估计的可靠性和推断问题，具有良好的频率学覆盖性质，适用于存在处理后混杂因素的复杂中介分析场景。

Abstract: We propose a Bayesian nonparametric approach using a truncated Enriched Dirichlet Process mixture (EDPM) model to estimate natural direct (NDE) and indirect (NIE) effects in causal mediation analyses in the presence of post-treatment confounders. We introduce an efficient cluster reallocation Metropolis-Hasting algorithm to improve mixing in the blocked Gibbs sampler. We implement a one-step posterior correction based on the efficient influence function for our setting. This post-processing step solves a critical problem in Bayesian nonparametrics: how to obtain reliable estimates and posteriors for a specific causal estimand of interest (the NDE and NIE) with excellent frequentist properties, such as correct coverage, from a model designed for complex joint distributions. We conduct simulation studies to assess our method's performance and apply it to evaluate causal mediation effects in a weight management clinical trial.

</details>


### [168] [Analysis of uncertain fixed-effects model for Latin square designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20226)
*Yaru Cheng,Zhiming Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对实验设计中缺乏频率稳定性的不确定数据，基于不确定测度建立了拉丁方设计的不确定固定效应模型，提出了三种估计方法及其置信区间，并给出了假设检验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典固定效应模型仅能处理精确的实验数据，但实验设计中经常出现缺乏频率稳定性的不确定数据，需要新的模型来处理这类数据。

Method: 基于不确定测度（uncertain measure），建立拉丁方设计的不确定固定效应模型；提出三种不确定估计方法处理效应和区组效应，并构建置信区间；进行不确定齐性检验和通用检验以评估处理效应的显著性。

Result: 通过数值模拟，从偏差、均方误差、平均绝对误差、整体标准差、覆盖概率和平均区间长度六个指标对三种估计方法进行了比较。通过若干算例展示了估计与假设检验的流程，并在真实教育数据上验证了模型的实用价值。

Conclusion: 不确定固定效应模型能够有效处理拉丁方设计中的不确定数据，具有实际应用价值，尤其在教育等领域的实验数据分析中表现出良好的适用性。

Abstract: Uncertain data without frequency stability often arises in experimental design. Classical fixed-effects models can only analyze precise experimental data. Based on an uncertain measure, this paper establishes uncertain fixed-effect models for Latin-square designs. First, we propose three methods with uncertainty to estimate the treatment and blocked effects and construct their confidence intervals. Then, uncertain homogeneity and common tests are conducted to assess the significance of treatment effects. In the numerical simulations, the three estimation methods are compared based on bias, mean squared error, mean absolute error, overall standard deviation, coverage probability, and average interval length. Several examples are given to illustrate the process of estimation and hypothesis. Finally, the uncertain fixed-effects model is applied to real education data, demonstrating its practical value.

</details>


### [169] [Anchors Away: Navigating Unanchored Indirect Comparisons with Multilevel Unanchored Meta-Regression (ML-UMR)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20341)
*Conor Chandler,Jack Ishak*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为多水平非锚定Meta回归（ML-UMR）的贝叶斯回归框架，用于在缺乏随机对照试验证据的情况下，整合来自完全脱节证据的个体患者数据和聚合数据，以进行多治疗、多研究的间接比较，并支持将结果外推至不同目标人群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当随机对照证据不可得时，HTA中越来越多地使用非锚定间接比较，但现有方法（如MAIC、STC）局限于成对比较，且通常估计的是对照研究人群的边际效应，而非决策相关人群的效应。需要一种能同时处理多治疗、多研究，并能灵活设定目标人群的统一框架。

Method: ML-UMR是ML-NMR在非锚定情境下的扩展，构建了一个统一的贝叶斯回归似然框架，联合建模个体级数据和聚合级数据。该框架明确区分了识别治疗效应所需的假设（如条件可交换性、结果模型正确设定、跨治疗假设如共享预后因子假设SPFA）与将结果外推至目标人群所需的假设，并支持敏感性分析。

Result: 模拟研究表明，ML-UMR在估计对照人群效应时具有低偏倚和名义覆盖率。然而，外推至其他人群的可传输性严重依赖于识别假设：当SPFA被违反且存在强效应修饰时，估计出现偏倚；但纳入亚组信息后，可恢复接近无偏的估计和名义覆盖率。

Conclusion: ML-UMR为完全脱节证据的非锚定间接比较提供了一个统一、灵活且透明的贝叶斯框架，虽然不能弱化所需的强假设，但通过使假设显式化并支持敏感性分析，增强了推断的可信度和在HTA中的实用性。

Abstract: Unanchored indirect treatment comparisons using single-arm studies or disconnected evidence are increasingly used in health technology assessment (HTA) when randomized evidence is unavailable. Existing methods, including matching-adjusted indirect comparison (MAIC) and simulated treatment comparison (STC), are generally limited to pairwise settings and typically estimate marginal effects in the comparator study population, which may differ from the decision-relevant population.
  We propose multilevel unanchored meta-regression (ML-UMR), a Bayesian regression framework for synthesizing individual patient data and aggregate data from fully disconnected evidence. ML-UMR extends multilevel network meta-regression (ML-NMR) to unanchored settings by jointly modeling individual- and aggregate-level data within a unified likelihood, enabling estimation of treatment-specific outcomes and both marginal and conditional effects across multiple treatments, studies, and target populations.
  ML-UMR distinguishes assumptions required to identify treatment effects from those required to transport results to target populations. As with all unanchored comparisons, valid inference relies on strong and often unverifiable assumptions, including conditional exchangeability, correct specification of the outcome model, and cross-treatment assumptions (e.g., shared prognostic factor assumption (SPFA)). ML-UMR does not lessen these requirements but makes them explicit within a unified framework and facilitates sensitivity analyses.
  In simulation studies, ML-UMR produced low bias and nominal coverage for comparator-population effects. Transportability to alternative populations depended critically on identifying assumptions: violations of SPFA led to bias under strong effect modification, whereas incorporating subgroup information restored near-unbiased estimation and nominal coverage.

</details>


### [170] [Flexible modeling of bimodal distributions via skewed-$t$ mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20406)
*Marco Bee,Flavio Santi*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于位置-尺度偏态t分布的混合模型，用于拟合双峰、偏态和重尾数据。模型采用Fernández和Steel (1998)的偏态t分布作为基础分布，通过EM算法进行极大似然估计，并构建了偏态性似然比检验。模拟研究表明该模型在估计精度和建模能力上优于混合g-and-h分布。应用于标普500指数数据，证实了其分布的双峰性，反映美股市场历史上主要处于熊市或牛市状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融收益率等实际数据常常同时呈现双峰性、偏态性和重尾性，传统单一分布族难以有效捕捉这些特征。现有混合模型在灵活性和推断便利性方面存在局限，因此需要一种既能灵活建模、又便于统计推断的混合分布框架。

Method: 以Fernández和Steel (1998)的位置-尺度偏态t分布为成分分布构建混合模型；研究混合分布的统计性质；基于EM算法开发极大似然估计方法；构造检验各成分偏态性是否为零的似然比检验；通过模拟实验与混合g-and-h分布进行对比评估。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在正确设定的情形下，所提模型具有优异的估计精度；在错误设定的框架下，也展现出良好的建模能力，整体表现优于混合g-and-h分布。对标普500指数数据的实证分析确认了其分布的双峰特征。

Conclusion: 所提出的偏态t混合模型能够有效刻画双峰、偏态和重尾数据，其建模框架可便捷地推广至其他对称分布的混合模型。实证结果支持美股市场在历史上主要处于熊市或牛市两种状态，而非围绕其基本面价值波动的观点。

Abstract: We propose a mixture of location-scale skewed-$t$ distributions to fit bimodal, skewed and heavy-tailed data. In particular, the mixture is based on the skewed-$t$ distribution by Fernández and Steel (1998), so that the model-building procedure can be easily extended to mixtures of other symmetric distributions. After studying the properties of the mixture, we develop a maximum likelihood estimation approach via the EM algorithm and a likelihood ratio test of the null hypothesis of no skewness in any given component. A simulation-based comparison to a recently proposed mixture of g-and-h distributions suggests that the performance of the proposed model is excellent, in terms of both estimation precision in well-specified setups and modeling capability in mis-specified frameworks. Fitting the model to the Standard & Poor's 500 distortion allows us to confirm the bimodality of its distribution, with the implication that the US stock market has historically been in bearish or bullish conditions, rather than near its fundamental value.

</details>


### [171] [Hypergraph Variable Selection with False Discovery Rate Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20514)
*Sarah Organ,Toby Kenney,Hong Gu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于超图的变量选择方法，通过定义在预测变量集合上的广义假发现率（FDR），在控制FDR的同时，克服了传统方法在复杂相关结构下功效不足的问题，并在多种场景下展现出更高的统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制假发现率（FDR）的变量选择方法在预测变量呈现复杂依赖结构时往往功效不足。作者先前的工作表明，层次聚类分组可以在保持FDR控制的同时缓解这一问题，但当相关性结构不够清晰时，重叠的预测变量集合可能更为有效。因此，需要一种能够处理非层次化、重叠结构的变量选择新框架。

Method: 作者首先将假发现率的概念推广到定义在预测变量集合上的假设检验，建立广义假发现率框架。在此基础上，提出了一种基于超图（hypergraph）的选择方法：将预测变量建模为超图中的节点，允许以重叠的集合（超边）形式组织变量，从而灵活捕捉复杂的依赖结构，并在该框架下实现严格的FDR控制。

Result: 所提出的超图方法在多种模拟场景中均表现出更高的统计功效（power），同时在所有设置下保持了严格的假发现率控制。

Conclusion: 基于超图的广义假发现率框架能够有效应对预测变量间复杂且非结构化的相关性，在保持FDR控制的前提下显著提升变量选择的统计功效，为高维数据中的变量选择问题提供了一种更加灵活且严谨的解决方案。

Abstract: Variable selection methods that control the false discovery rate often lose power when predictors exhibit complex dependence structures. We previously showed that selecting hierarchically clustered groups of predictors can mitigate this issue while maintaining false discovery rate control. When correlations are less structured, however, overlapping predictor sets may be more effective. We introduce a generalized false discovery rate for hypotheses defined on sets of predictors and propose a hypergraph-based selection method. This approach achieves higher power across diverse settings while preserving rigorous false discovery rate control.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Deontic Policies for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19464)
*Anupam Joshi,Tim Finin,Karuna Pande Joshi,Lalana Kagal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticRei框架，基于道义逻辑和OWL本体，在LLM之外实现完整的AI智能体治理，涵盖许可/禁止、义务管理、豁免、策略冲突解决及领域本体推理，弥补了XACML、Rego、Cedar等现有策略引擎的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自主智能体系统带来了超越传统认证与访问控制的安全、隐私与合规挑战。现有策略引擎（XACML、Rego、Cedar）仅覆盖许可/禁止子集，缺乏义务生命周期管理、元策略冲突解决、特定条件下的义务豁免以及基于领域本体的推理能力，无法满足企业治理的全方位需求。

Method: 提出AgenticRei框架，采用基于Rei框架的道义策略语言，以OWL（Web本体语言）表达策略，并在LLM外部部署高性能逻辑引擎进行运行时评估。该管道同时治理智能体的工具调用和智能体间消息交互，并可与A2AS等行业标准框架自然组合。

Result: 通过示例表明，道义策略能够捕捉当前生产级策略引擎几乎无法表达的安全与隐私治理约束，覆盖义务、豁免、冲突解决与领域本体推理等关键治理需求。

Conclusion: AgenticRei通过引入义务、豁免、元策略冲突解决和本体推理等机制，将AI智能体治理从简单的许可/禁止模型扩展为完整的企业治理结构，为安全关键场景中的自主智能体系统提供了可行的策略执行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic AI systems driven by Large Language Models (LLMs) introduce a new class of security, privacy, and compliance challenges: an agent that can invoke tools, manipulate data, install software, and coordinate with peer agents across organizational boundaries must be constrained not just by authentication and access control, but by the full structure of enterprise governance. This includes specifying what agents are permitted and prohibited from doing, what they areobliged to do after certain actions (e.g., notify the CISO), under what conditions a standing obligation may be waived, and which rules take precedence when policies conflict. This governance problem exceeds what current policy engines provide. Systems such as XACML, Rego, and Cedar address only the permit/prohibit subset of this governance structure. They do not provide obligation lifecycle management, meta-policy conflict resolution, dispensations that waive obligations in specific circumstances, and ontological reasoning over domain class hierarchies commonly found in applications such as healthcare, cybersecurity, or data privacy. We propose AgenticRei, which realizes key governance requirements such as obligations, dispensations, policy conflict resolutions, and reasoning over policies, as well as the basic permit/prohibit constraints. We use a deontic policy language built on the Rei framework, expressed as OWL (Web Ontology Language) and evaluated at runtime by a high-performance logic engine entirely outside the LLM. The same pipeline governs both tool invocations by the agent and agent-to-agent messages. We show through examples that deontic policies capture governance constraints around security and privacy that mostly cannot be expressed in current production engines. Our approach composes naturally with industry-standard frameworks like A2AS.

</details>


### [173] [Measuring Curriculum Alignment across Topical Coverage, Competency, and Cognitive Depth: A Longitudinal Framework Applied to CS2013 and CS2023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19469)
