Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.

</details>


### [242] [LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20529)
*Md Nayem Uddin,Amir Saeidi,Eduardo Blanco,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LedgerAgent 提出一种推理时方法，通过独立的"账本"显式维护任务状态，并在工具调用前进行状态依赖的策略约束检查，从而减少客服场景中工具调用智能体的状态管理失败与策略违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准工具调用智能体将观测、工具返回和策略指令全部堆入提示中，任务状态隐式存在，导致两类常见失败：(1) 智能体基于陈旧、缺失或错误信息做出决策；(2) 语法正确的工具调用仍可能违反依赖当前任务状态的领域策略。需要在客服等策略严苛的场景中显式管理状态并阻断违规。

Method: LedgerAgent 在推理时采用两个核心机制：(1) 维护一个独立的"账本"（Ledger），持续记录从用户交互和工具调用中观测到的任务状态（事实、标识符、约束、条件等），并将账本中的状态渲染到提示中；(2) 在执行会改变环境的工具调用之前，利用账本检查状态依赖的策略约束，若违反则阻止调用。该方法可与任意基础模型配合，无需额外训练。

Result: 在四个客服领域和一组开源/闭源模型混合评测中，LedgerAgent 对比标准基于提示的工具调用方法，平均 pass^k 指标提升，尤其在更严格的多轮一致性指标下增益最大。

Conclusion: 显式的任务状态管理（账本）结合调用前的策略约束检查，能有效缓解客服工具调用智能体的状态漂移与策略违规问题，对多轮一致性和可靠性要求高的场景尤为有益。

Abstract: Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.

</details>


### [243] [How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20532)
*Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Wasim Madha,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次将DAAM交叉注意力归因框架迁移至语音扩散模型，在CapSpeech-TTS上分析风格描述文本中每个token如何影响声学输出，揭示了风格token的全局条件作用、注意力与F0/能量的相关性、以及深层早期步骤中风格条件作用的峰值等规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风格描述驱动的TTS系统中，自然语言如何逐词影响声学输出尚不明确，理解这一机制对于诊断失败模式和提升可控性至关重要。

Method: 提出面向语音扩散模型的交叉注意力归因方法，将DAAM框架首次适配到语音领域。在CapSpeech-TTS上对25层、24个ODE步骤提取逐token热力图，分析3600组（120条风格描述 × 30条文本）的风格-内容组合。

Result: (1) 风格token的时间方差低于内容/功能词，确证其全局条件作用；(2) 风格注意力与F0和能量相关；(3) 风格条件作用在早期扩散步骤和深层网络中达到峰值；(4) 注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，与风格重要性峰值共现，表明在最关键风格阶段网络选择性最强。

Conclusion: 首次系统揭示了自然语言在语音扩散模型中影响交叉注意力机制的方式，为提升富有表现力的TTS系统的可控性与可解释性提供了理论和实证基础。

Abstract: Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models

</details>


### [244] [Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20544)
*Gina Wong,Drew Prinster,Suchi Saria,Rama Chellappa,Anqi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合专家模型（MoE）中专家级校准与整体模型校准之间的关系，发现在硬路由下专家校准足以保证分布偏移下的整体校准，但软路由下不足以保证。为此，作者提出了一种对抗性重加权方法，以改善分布偏移下的准确率-校准权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 校准对于理解和信任模型的预测概率至关重要。尽管已有研究表明在个体预测器层面强制校准则能提升集成模型（尤其是MoE）的准确率和校准效果，但在何种条件下校准对MoE有益尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统研究MoE在分布偏移下的校准行为。

Method: （1）理论分析：分别对硬路由（hard-routed）和软路由（soft-routed）MoE模型，在分布偏移下分析专家级校准与整体模型校准的关系，证明专家校准对硬路由充分、对软路由不充分。（2）提出方法：设计一种对抗性重加权（adversarial reweighting）方法，在分布偏移下惩罚路由聚合后的校准误差，从而优化整体模型的校准性能。

Result: 对抗性重加权方法在多种模型类别、预测任务和分布偏移场景下，均能在平均性能和困难数据子集上改善准确率与校准之间的权衡，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 专家级校准在硬路由MoE中足以保证分布偏移下的整体校准，但在软路由中不足；所提出的对抗性重加权方法能有效解决软路由MoE在校准上的不足，显著提升分布偏移下的校准-准确率权衡。

Abstract: Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [Computational Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19361)
*Lucius E. J. Bynum,Rajesh Ranganath,Kyunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"计算可识别性"框架，将传统理论可识别性（依赖渐近假设和无限数据）重新定义为有限计算搜索过程：若在给定误差容忍范围内能经验地找到估计量，则满足可识别性。该框架可处理小样本、模糊图准则、混合观测-干预数据以及反事实估计等实际识别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断中的理论可识别性依赖渐近性质、无限数据等理想化条件，难以回答有限样本、模糊因果图、混合数据类型等实际场景中的细粒度识别问题。作者旨在弥合理论可识别性与实际计算可行性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出"计算可识别性"框架，将可识别性定义为：在给定参数先验分布和搜索过程的假设下，若一个有限计算搜索过程能在期望误差容忍范围内经验地找到估计量，则可识别性得到满足。该框架通过计算搜索过程替代理论推导，并在多个实验场景中验证其有效性。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能够回答传统方法难以处理的识别问题，包括：小有限样本下的识别、模糊图准则下的识别、混合观测-干预数据的识别，以及反事实数据和估计量的识别。

Conclusion: 计算可识别性为因果推断提供了一种更实用、更细粒度的识别判定方式，将可识别性从理论理想条件拓展到有限计算和有限数据的实际场景，为小样本和复杂数据条件下的因果效应估计开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Identification conditions describe the computability of a target query or parameter of interest as a function of the type and amount of information available. In causal identification, this information is often expressed in the form of a causal graph, and data are observed or collected for some subset of variables in the graph. Target queries may be for a single effect alone or for a class of effects in a given model. The derivation of an identification algorithm then defines mathematically the process by which the desired causal effect(s) can be uniquely determined, theoretically, in expectation. Identifiability in expectation, or 'theoretical identifiability,' generally assumes asymptotic properties, infinite data, or other mathematically idealized conditions. In this paper, we explore a fundamental distinction between this theoretical, idealized notion of identifiability and a proposed alternative that is computation-bound. The framework we propose - 'computational identifiability' - is to instead define a finite computational search procedure for an empirical estimator. If this process finds an estimator empirically, within a desired error tolerance, then identifiability is satisfied, conditional on the specified assumptions of the search (i.e., a prior distribution over the parameters) and conditional on the search procedure itself. Through several experiments, we demonstrate how this framework allows us to answer fine-grained, practical identification questions, such as identification with small finite samples, with ambiguous graphical criteria, with mixed observational-interventional data, and across counterfactual data and estimands. Code is available at https://github.com/lbynum/metadentify.

</details>


### [246] [When to Trust, How to Distill: Multi-Foundation Model Guidance for Lightweight, Robust Scientific Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19363)
*Rupasree Dey,Abdul Matin,Nathan Orwick,Yao Zhang,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GUARD框架，通过上下文路由和不确定性门控温度机制，将多个分布失配的时序基础模型知识蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，在气候科学领域实现高精度边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序基础模型（TSFMs）虽编码丰富的通用时序动态，但零样本应用于特定科学领域时存在严重的分布失配问题，且计算成本过高无法部署于边缘传感器网络。核心挑战在于：如何从这些失配的基础模型中提取潜在结构知识，训练轻量级、专业化的预测器。

Method: 提出GUARD（Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation）框架，将多教师蒸馏重构为实例级决策过程，包含两个自适应机制：(1) 上下文路由器（Contextual Router），基于局部输入统计动态选择最相关教师，利用不同基础模型间的互补性；(2) 不确定性门控温度机制（Uncertainty-Gated Temperature），当教师置信度与领域真实情况偏离时自动衰减蒸馏强度，起到"断路器"作用。

Result: 在气象学、生态系统碳通量、土壤湿度和能源电网四个气候关键领域进行评估。相比固定权重的多教师蒸馏基线，RMSE显著降低。即使教师模型因分布偏移而零样本精度次优，仍能成功蒸馏知识；分布失配的教师在28.5%的最难实例上表现优于全局最优的基础模型，起到关键的纠偏作用。

Conclusion: GUARD证明分布失配的教师模型仍可作为有价值的纠正信号源，实现了适用于资源受限边缘部署的高精度科学预测，有效解决了基础模型向特定科学领域迁移的分布对齐与计算成本双重挑战。

Abstract: The deployment of Time-Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) in physical sciences is hindered by a critical trade-off: while these models encode rich, universal temporal dynamics, they suffer from severe distributional misalignment when applied zero-shot to specific scientific domains, and their computational cost prohibits deployment in edge-computing sensor networks. We address a fundamental challenge: How can we extract latent structural knowledge from misaligned foundation models (FM) to train lightweight, specialized forecasters? We propose Gated Uncertainty-Aware Routing for Distillation (Guard), a novel framework that reframes multiteacher distillation as an instance-wise decision process with two adaptive mechanisms: (1) a Contextual Router that dynamically selects the most relevant teacher based on local input statistics, exploiting complementarity across diverse foundation models; and (2) an Uncertainty-Gated Temperature mechanism that acts as a "circuit-breaker," automatically attenuating distillation strength when teacher confidence diverges from domain reality. We evaluate our proposed lightweight framework on four climate-critical domains: meteorology, ecosystem carbon flux, soil moisture, and energy grids. Our method significantly reduces RMSE relative to a fixed-weight multi-teacher distillation baseline, successfully distilling knowledge from pretrained FMs (teachers) even when they exhibit suboptimal zero-shot accuracy due to distribution shift between the original and target data domains. We demonstrate that these domain-misaligned teachers can still serve as critical correctives, outperforming the globally superior FMs on 28.5% of the hardest instances. Ultimately, this enables high-precision scientific forecasting suitable for resource-constrained edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/RupasreeDey/GUARD-KDD2026.

</details>


### [247] [Closing the Social-Semantic Gap: SPSD for Edge-Based Prompt Compression in Cloud LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19364)
*Abhinit Sen,Ajeet Kumar,Manaranjan Pradhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPSD是一种边缘端提示蒸馏流水线，利用4-bit量化的小语言模型在发送到云端LLM之前压缩用户提示中的社交性语言，实现了平均每调用节省99.9个输入token，同时保证响应质量在预设的非劣效边界内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的预填充阶段在云规模上能耗巨大，而许多对话提示中包含大量社交性语言（礼貌用语、道歉性开场白、重复性表达、关系构建语言等），这些对人类交流很重要但对机器推理的信息增量极低。作者将这种现象称为"社交-语义鸿沟"，旨在通过边缘侧预处理来降低云端推理成本。

Method: 提出SPSD（情感保持语义蒸馏）流水线：在边缘端部署一个4-bit量化的小语言模型（Gemma-2-2B-Instruct Q4_K_M），对用户提示进行语义蒸馏，去除社交性冗余但保留核心语义和情感倾向；蒸馏后的提示再传输到云端LLM（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）进行推理。系统还包含基于规则的安全敏感域门控机制，对高风险领域采用直通路由。

Result: 在248条提示语料上评估：所有146次蒸馏调用均实现正向token节省，平均节省99.9个token；121对盲法LLM-as-judge评分显示蒸馏路径在15分量表上非劣效于原始路径（预设1分边界），其中43%平局、28%蒸馏胜出、29%原始胜出；余弦相似度均值0.682、中位数0.712，54.1%的配对超过0.70参考阈值；每次调用净节能估计为70-270 uWh。

Conclusion: SPSD证明了在边缘端进行提示蒸馏可以在保持响应质量的前提下显著降低云端LLM的输入token成本，为降低云规模LLM推理能耗提供了一种实用方案。

Abstract: The prefill stage of Large Language Model (LLM) inference is a growing contributor to cloud-scale energy cost. Many consumer-support and conversational prompts contain social scaffolding: politeness markers, apologetic preamble, repetition, and rapport-building language that is important for human communication but carries low marginal information for machine reasoning. We call this discrepancy the Social-Semantic Gap. We present SPSD (Sentiment Preserving Semantic Distillation), an edge-based pipeline that compresses user prompts using a 4-bit quantised Small Language Model before transmission to a cloud-deployed LLM. Evaluation on a 248-prompt corpus using Gemma-2-2B-Instruct (Q4_K_M) as the SLM and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct as the cloud evaluation model yields a mean input token saving of 99.9 tokens per distilled call, with all 146 distilled calls yielding positive savings. Response quality, assessed by blind LLM-as-judge scoring across 121 pairs, is non-inferior to the raw path within a pre-specified 1-point margin on a 15-point rubric; the judge awarded 43 percent ties, 28 percent distilled wins, and 29 percent raw wins. Cosine similarity is mixed: mean 0.682, median 0.712, with 54.1 percent of pairs above the 0.70 reference threshold. Safety-critical domains are conservatively routed to passthrough via rule-based gates. Per-call net energy saving is estimated at 70-270 uWh under stated assumptions. SPSD shows that on-device prompt distillation can reduce cloud LLM input-token cost while preserving response quality within a practical non-inferiority margin.

</details>


### [248] [Performance Analysis and Optimization of 3D Generative Diffusion Models across GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19365)
*Jeeho Ryoo,Yongchan Jung,Muhammad Ali Khaliq,Weidong Zhang,Jiatong Han,Byeong Kil Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对医学扩散模型Med-DDPM在三代NVIDIA GPU上的性能瓶颈进行了深入分析，发现训练受限于cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM算子的内存访问模式与Tensor Core利用率低下问题，并提出TF32 Tensor Core激活与3D channels-last布局两项优化，实现最高100倍的SM周期缩减与Tensor Core利用率从1.45倍提升至9.98倍，且不影响合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在3D MRI合成中表现优异，但部署受限于高昂的GPU资源消耗（每样本数百次U-Net评估）和高度异构的核函数行为。当前缺乏对医学扩散模型在GPU架构层面的系统性能分析，难以定位瓶颈并指导优化。

Method: 以Med-DDPM为目标模型，在三代NVIDIA架构（涵盖A100等）上进行全面的核函数级性能剖析，包括：核函数运行时间分解、指令混合特征、内存系统利用率、Warp级活动以及Profiler优先级评分估计。基于分析结果，评估两项架构感知优化：TF32 Tensor Core激活与3D channels-last内存布局。

Result: 训练阶段主要被cuDNN卷积和隐式GEMM核函数占据，瓶颈在于内存访问模式不佳、张量布局转换开销大、Tensor Core利用率低。优化后：SM周期减少达100倍，动态指令数减少100倍，Tensor Core利用率从1.45倍提升至9.98倍，A100上IPC提升7%，且合成质量未下降。

Conclusion: 通过架构感知的核函数级剖析能够精准定位医学扩散模型的GPU性能瓶颈，TF32 Tensor Core与channels-last布局的组合优化可大幅提升训练效率，为3D医学图像合成在资源受限环境中的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become essential for high-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis, yet their deployment remains constrained by substantial GPU resource demands arising from hundreds of U-Net evaluations per sample and a highly heterogeneous kernel behavior. This paper performs a comprehensive performance analysis of the state-of-the-art medical diffusion model, Med-DDPM, across three generations of NVIDIA architectures to study kernel-level runtime breakdowns, instruction-mix characteristics, memory system utilization, warp-level activities, and profiler priority-score estimates. We show that training is overwhelmingly dominated by cuDNN convolution and implicit-GEMM kernels, with inefficiencies arising from memory-access patterns, tensor-layout conversions, and limited Tensor Core utilization. Guided by these insights, we evaluate two architecture-aware optimizations TF32 Tensor Core activation and a 3D channels-last layout and demonstrate that they reduce SM cycles by up to 100x, cut dynamic instructions by 100x, raise Tensor Core utilization from 1.45 to 9.98x, and increase IPC by 7% on A100, all without degrading synthesis quality.

</details>


### [249] [Information Lattice Learning as Probabilistic Graphical Model Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19366)
*Haizi Yu,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 信息格学习(ILL)通过将信号交替投影到编码抽象层次的划分格上，并将规则提升回信号域来学习可解释规则。本文揭示了ILL学习的概率规则与概率图模型之间的内在联系：划分诱导商变量，规则即商变量的边际律，规则集构成边际约束集合；一般提升是所有满足约束的联合分布族，特殊提升采用L2均匀性准则实现最大无知重建。在Shannon熵提升下，约束等价于对数线性因子图。信息格本身不是贝叶斯网络，其边编码的是抽象的细化/粗化关系，而非条件依赖。因此ILL本质上是一种基于商变量的可解释约束型因子图的结构学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息格学习(ILL)虽能有效学习可解释规则，但其概率论基础及其与概率图模型(PGM)的关系尚未被充分阐明。作者旨在建立ILL与PGM之间的形式化联系，从而为ILL提供坚实的概率解释框架，并揭示其在结构学习中的定位。

Method: 本文采用形式化分析方法：(1)将ILL中的划分映射为确定性商变量，将规则定义为商变量的边际分布；(2)将规则集解释为一组边际约束；(3)区分一般提升(所有满足约束的联合分布族)和特殊提升(基于L2均匀性准则的最大无知重建)；(4)将Shannon熵提升下的约束等价为对数线性因子图模型；(5)通过对比信息格的边(编码细化/粗化)与贝叶斯网络的边(编码条件依赖)，阐明ILL与贝叶斯网络的本质区别。

Result: ILL的概率规则自然对应PGM结构：划分→商变量，规则→边际律，规则集→边际约束集合。L2均匀性准则与最大熵原理密切相关。Shannon熵提升下约束条件产生因子由图中所学抽象索引的对数线性因子图。信息格的边语义为细化/粗化而非条件依赖，因此ILL不是贝叶斯网络，而是针对商变量的可解释约束型因子图的结构学习方法。

Conclusion: ILL应被理解为一种面向商变量、基于约束的可解释因子图结构学习方法，而非贝叶斯网络。这一视角厘清了ILL与图模型及最大熵模型的关系，并为推断、可辨识性以及符号-概率混合学习等方向开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Information lattice learning (ILL) learns interpretable rules of a signal by alternately projecting the signal onto a partition lattice that encodes a hierarchy of abstractions and lifting selected rules back to the signal domain. When the signal is a probability mass function, we show the probabilistic rules learned by ILL admit a natural probabilistic graphical model (PGM) interpretation and develop this interpretation in detail. A partition in ILL induces a deterministic quotient variable, and a rule is the marginal law of that quotient variable. A rule set is therefore a collection of marginal constraints over interpretable abstractions. General lifting is the feasible family of all joint distributions satisfying those constraints, while special lifting chooses a maximum-ignorance reconstruction, implemented in ILL by an L2 uniformity principle closely related to maximum entropy. Under a Shannon-entropy lifting, the same constraints yield a log-linear factor graph whose factors are indexed by learned abstractions. The information lattice itself, however, is not a Bayesian network: its edges encode refinement and coarsening of abstractions, not conditional dependence. Thus ILL is best viewed as structure learning for interpretable constraint-based factor graphs over quotient variables. This view clarifies how ILL relates to graphical models and maximum entropy models, while suggesting new directions for inference, identifiability, and hybrid symbolic-probabilistic learning.

</details>


### [250] [Weibull Weight-Scale Parameter Evolution under AdamW Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19367)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于双参数Weibull框架诊断Transformer权重分布，研究了AdamW训练过程中Weibull尺度参数λ的增长、超调与松弛现象。作者将权重范数平方分解为对齐力、注入力和衰减力三个分量，解释了λ的动态演化机制，并提出样条位移法在无优化器力矩条件下恢复对齐力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdamW训练中Transformer权重分布尺度参数λ的动力学行为及其背后的驱动力机制，揭示权重增长、超调和松弛的内在原因。

Method: 从AdamW更新公式出发，推导权重范数平方的主导阶三力分解：对齐力（权重与自适应更新方向的相关性）、注入力（自适应步长幅度）和衰减力（解耦权重衰减）。在自训练的Pythia-70M模型上进行验证，使用真实优化器力矩。进一步提出样条位移法，在仅有稀疏checkpoint条件下恢复对齐力（准确率约92-94%）。

Result: 对齐力在λ上升阶段占主导，贡献绝对力预算的88-94%，且对超权重移除具有鲁棒性。接近饱和时，对齐力与衰减力趋于平衡，解释了从增长到松弛的转变。RMS到Weibull的重构偏移可分解为桥接分量和积分分量，在密集采样区域总计约5-6%。样条位移法恢复对齐力的准确率约为朴素两点基线的两倍。λ(t)峰值随训练数据一致性变化。

Conclusion: AdamW训练中权重尺度参数λ的动力学由对齐力、注入力和衰减力三力共同支配，其中对齐力是增长阶段的主导机制。该框架为理解自适应优化器下的权重分布演化提供了新的理论工具，样条位移法则拓展了实际应用场景的分析能力。

Abstract: Building on a two-parameter Weibull framework for diagnosing transformer weight distributions, we study why the Weibull weight-scale parameter $λ$ grows, overshoots, and then relaxes during AdamW training. We derive a leading-order three-force decomposition of the squared weight norm from the AdamW update: an alignment force measuring the correlation between weights and the adaptive update direction, an injection force from adaptive step magnitude, and a decay force from decoupled weight decay. On self-trained Pythia-70M models with ground-truth optimizer moments, alignment dominates the rise phase, contributing 88-94% of the absolute force budget across four random seeds and remaining robust to super-weight removal. Near saturation, alignment and decay approach balance, explaining the transition from weight-scale growth to relaxation. These force dynamics directly govern the squared-norm component underlying $λ(t)$; the remaining RMS-to-Weibull reconstruction offset is measurable and decomposes into bridge and integration components, totaling approximately 5-6% in densely sampled regions. To extend the analysis to real models where optimizer moments are unavailable, we introduce a spline displacement method that recovers the alignment force from sparse checkpoints with approximately 92-94% accuracy, about twice the naive two-point baseline. We further observe that the peak value of $λ(t)$ varies with training-data coherence in our experiments, suggesting a data-dependent component of weight-scale growth that we leave to a controlled follow-up study. Code and data are available at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public.

</details>


### [251] [Zero-Inflated Gaussian Distributions Enable Parameter-Space Sparsity in Estimation-of-Distribution Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19369)
*Andreas Faust,Sven Nitzsche,Juergen Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多元零膨胀高斯分布作为分布估计算法(EDA)的采样分布，首次将EDA推广到稀疏参数空间，避免手工设计稀疏算子，实现稀疏模式与活跃值的联合优化，在Lunar Lander基准上优于多种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EDA在连续参数空间表现良好，但未推广到稀疏空间；现有稀疏黑盒优化器重新引入手工设计的稀疏算子、双层方案和零化阈值，违背了EDA避免手工设计的初衷。

Method: 采用多元零膨胀高斯(ZIG)分布作为EDA采样分布，通过潜变量高斯模型同时表示稀疏模式、活跃参数间相关性及二者交互；证明模型参数可从观测样本中识别，并引入基于摊销反演的实用估计器，实现对潜变量相关结构的精确恢复。

Result: 在Lunar Lander基准上，ZIG-EDA比稠密高斯EDA、手工稀疏进化算法和临时稀疏EDA收敛更快、最终回报更高，且仅需少量活跃参数。

Conclusion: ZIG-EDA成功将EDA范式拓展到稀疏优化领域，无需手工稀疏算子即可实现层次无关的联合优化，为稀疏黑盒优化提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Estimation-of-distribution algorithms (EDAs) are a powerful class of evolutionary methods for black-box optimization, especially when little is known about the structure of the objective. Whereas classical evolutionary algorithms rely on hand-designed mutation and crossover operators, hard to devise for unknown problem structures, and a source of bias, EDAs sidestep operator design entirely: they fit a probability distribution to the best individuals and sample the next generation from it. EDAs are well established on continuous parameter spaces, but they have not previously been generalized to sparse ones, in which most coefficients of a good solution are exactly zero. Existing sparse black-box optimizers therefore reintroduce exactly what EDAs were designed to avoid: hand-crafted sparsity operators, bi-level schemes alternating between support set and active values, zeroing thresholds, and other baked-in assumptions. We close this gap by proposing multivariate zero-inflated Gaussian (ZIG) distributions as EDA sampling laws. A latent Gaussian model with separate indicator and value dimensions represents sparsity patterns, correlations among active parameters, and the interactions between the two, so sparsity patterns and active values are optimized jointly, hierarchy-free. We show that the latent parameters of this model are identifiable from observed samples, unlike in the missing-data settings where related constructions originate, and introduce practical amortized inversion-based estimators for them. The estimators accurately recover latent correlation structures, and on the Lunar Lander benchmark the resulting ZIG-EDA converges faster and reaches higher final returns than a dense Gaussian EDA, a hand-crafted sparse evolutionary algorithm, and an ad-hoc sparse EDA, while finding controllers with only a small fraction of parameters active.

</details>


### [252] [Human-like autonomy emerges from self-play and a pinch of human data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19370)
*Daphne Cornelisse,Julian Hunt,Zixu Zhang,Waël Doulazmi,Kevin Joseph,Jaime Fernández Fisac,Eugene Vinitsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合少量人类示范数据的自博弈强化学习方法，仅需30分钟人类驾驶数据（比模仿学习少2500倍）作为正则化项，在最小安全目标达成奖励上训练，即可生成与人类驾驶习惯兼容的策略，训练仅需15小时（单张消费级GPU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯自博弈强化学习训练驾驶策略存在关键局限：策略虽能学会有效驾驶，但会形成与人类不兼容的异类驾驶习惯（alien driving conventions）。现有解决方案依赖大量奖励工程和领域随机化，既脆弱又耗时。作者试图以极少量人类数据来有效缓解行为偏差。

Method: 作者将人类示范数据作为正则化目标（regularization objective），叠加在最小化的安全目标达成奖励之上，而非完全替代奖励或完全依赖模仿学习。这种设计使得少量人类数据（仅30分钟示范）即可引导策略向着与人类兼容的方向收敛。

Result: 该方法仅需30分钟人类示范（比同类模仿学习方法少约2500倍），训练出的策略可与留出的人类轨迹协调配合，且整个训练流程在单张消费级GPU上仅需15小时即可完成。

Conclusion: “少量人类数据如佳肴中的香料，少即是多”——极少量人类示范作为正则化即可有效弥合自博弈策略与人类驾驶习惯之间的偏差，实现高效、低成本且行为对齐的自动驾驶策略训练。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has recently emerged as a way to train driving policies without any human data. It uses cheap, large-scale simulations to substitute expensive, large-scale human driving demonstrations. A key limitation of this approach is that policies trained through pure self-play can learn effective but alien driving conventions incompatible with people. Previous works attempt to mitigate such behavioral misalignments through extensive reward engineering and domain randomization, which are brittle and labor-intensive. Instead of completely discarding human demonstrations, our method treats them as a regularization objective on top of a minimal safe goal-reaching reward. Like the spice in a good stew, we find that a little human data goes a long way: our method uses only 30 minutes of human demonstrations, 2500x fewer than comparable imitation learning approaches. Resulting policies coordinate with held-out human trajectories and complete training in 15 hours on a single consumer-grade GPU. Videos and full source code are available at https://spiced-self-play.com/.

</details>


### [253] [ProMUSE: Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty-guided Staged Evidential Alzheimer Disease Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19371)
*Long Doan,Branden Chen,Ethan Litton,Huan Huang,Jiajing Huang,Yixin Xie,Weihua Zhou,Nandakumar Narayanan,Chen Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProMUSE 提出一种渐进式多模态不确定性引导的分阶段证据网络，通过自适应判断是否需要额外引入MRI/PET影像，在保持诊断精度的同时大幅降低数据采集成本，实现资源高效的阿尔茨海默病筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病早期诊断需依赖多模态数据（临床评估、MRI、PET），但MRI和PET采集成本高且并非普遍可及，实际临床中难以实现全模态推理，因此需要一种可根据不确定性自适应决定是否引入昂贵模态的策略，以降低整体成本。

Method: 提出ProMUSE框架：(1) 首先利用低成本临床数据进行证据分类，通过基于Dirichlet的主观逻辑模型量化预测不确定性；(2) 当不确定性超过学习到的阈值时，逐步引入MRI或PET特征；(3) 利用Dempster-Shafer理论融合各模态的信念与不确定性，得到校准后的多模态预测结果。

Result: 在ADNI、AIBL和OASIS数据集上的CN-AD、CN-MCI、MCI-AD三类任务中，ProMUSE在减少50-90%的MRI/PET使用量的情况下，达到与全模态基线相当甚至更优的准确率，实现显著的成本节省。

Conclusion: ProMUSE是一种实用、不确定性感知且资源高效的AD筛查方案，在真实临床工作流程中具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) is a fatal disorder that destroys memory and cognitive skills in the elderly population. Most treatments for AD are effective in the early stage, leading to an increasing demand for early AD diagnosis. AD diagnosis increasingly relies on multimodal data such as clinical assessments, structural Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI), and Positron Emission Tomography (PET) imaging. However, MRI and PET acquisition remain costly and not universally accessible, making full-modality inference impractical in real-world clinical workflows. We propose ProMUSE, a Progressive Multi-modal Uncertainty Guided Staged Evidential Network that adaptively determines when additional modalities are necessary, helping reduce the overall cost of data acquisition while maintaining accuracy. ProMUSE first performs evidential classification using low-cost clinical data and quantifies uncertainty via a Dirichlet-based subjective logic model. When uncertainty exceeds a learned threshold, ProMUSE progressively incorporates MRI or PET features, fusing modality-wise belief and uncertainty through Dempster-Shafer theory to obtain a calibrated multimodal prediction. This staged acquisition strategy enables accurate diagnosis while minimizing reliance on expensive imaging. Experiments on ADNI, AIBL, and OASIS across CN-AD, CN-MCI, and MCI-AD tasks demonstrate that ProMUSE achieves competitive or superior accuracy compared to full-modality baselines while reducing MRI/PET usage by 50-90%, yielding substantial cost savings. These results highlight ProMUSE as a practical, uncertainty-aware, and resource-efficient solution for real-world AD screening.

</details>


### [254] [cAPM: Continual AI-Assisted Pace-Mapping with Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19373)
*Dylan O'Hara,Pradeep Bajracharya,Casey Meisenzahl,Karli Gillette,Anton J. Prassl,Gernot Plank,Saman Nazarian,Roderick Tung,John L Sapp,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了cAPM（continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping），一种结合主动学习和持续学习策略的AI辅助起搏标测方法。通过任务无关的代理神经网络学习起搏位点到12导联心电图形态的映射，并利用持续学习在多个VT目标间迁移知识，显著减少了定位所需起搏位点数量（4.5个位点即达81%的临床精度概率，而现有方法需13.7个位点仅38%概率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助起搏标测方法针对每个VT目标需从头重新训练，无法在同一患者或跨患者的不同VT之间迁移知识，导致效率受限。需要一种能够积累并迁移过往起搏标测知识的持续学习方法，以减少未来VT目标定位所需的起搏位点数量。

Method: cAPM方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 任务无关的代理神经网络，学习从起搏位点到12导联ECG形态的映射；(2) 主动学习策略，为每个VT目标选择信息量最大的起搏位点以优化代理模型；(3) 持续学习策略，在连续处理多个VT目标时保留先前知识。在包含不同生理条件和心室几何形态的in-silico测试平台上进行验证，并对比了有无过往数据回放的cAPM变体。

Result: 在in-silico测试平台上，cAPM（含回放）使用平均4.5个起搏位点即达到81%的临床精度（5mm内）定位概率；相比之下，最先进的主动学习方法使用13.7个起搏位点仅达到38%概率，性能提升显著。

Conclusion: cAPM通过持续学习有效迁移跨VT的知识，大幅降低起搏标测所需数据量并提升效率，为推进至临床前和临床体内研究奠定了坚实基础，有望用于指导实际导管消融术中的起搏标测。

Abstract: Ventricular tachycardia is a life-threatening rhythm disorder and a major cause of sudden cardiac death. Pace-mapping is a clinical procedure for identifying the intervention target during catheter ablation of VT. It requires clinicians to pace different sites in the ventricles and rapidly interpret the resulting electrocardiograms to determine where to pace next or whether a target site has been identified. Active learning AI models have been proposed to guide clinicians to the next pacing site, showing promise in reducing the number of pacing sites and improving the efficiency of pace-mapping. Existing methods require retraining each target without the ability to transfer knowledge across multiple VTs within the same patient or across patients. We introduce cAPM for continuous AI-assisted pace-mapping to capture and transfer knowledge accumulated from past pace-mapping data to reduce the number of pace-mapping data needed for future target VTs. This is made possible by a task-agnostic surrogate neural network that learns the mapping from pacing sites to 12-lead ECG morphology, an active-learning strategy that refines this surrogate model by selecting the most informative pacing site for each target, and a continual learning strategy to do so sequentially while retaining knowledge from prior targets. Evaluated on an in-silico testbed consisting of sequentially-presented localization tasks across different physiological conditions and ventricular geometries, cAPM with and without replay of past data samples achieved an 81% probability of localizing within clinical tolerance (5 mm accuracy) using 4.5 pace-mapping sites, compared to the state-of-the-art active-learning method achieving 38% probability using 13.7 pacing sites. These results provide a strong basis for preparing cAPM towards in-vivo preclinical and clinical studies where it can be used to guide pace-mapping.

</details>


### [255] [Algebraic Dead Directions in LayerNorm Transformers: A Forward-Pass-Only Diagnostic at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19491)
*Tejas Pradeep Shirodkar,P. J. Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现：对于LayerNorm Transformer，LayerNorm仿射参数γ的逆缩放方向γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖精确等于最终归一化后中心化激活协方差的代数核，对应参数空间中的一个"死方向"（Fisher信息矩阵在该方向退化为零）。该方向可直接从LayerNorm的尺度参数读取，无需前向/反向传播或特征分解，是目前最廉价的死方向定位方法。在14个预训练Transformer上验证了该发现，并推导出两个闭式推论：残差流最小奇异值逐块保持性，以及仅凭参数即可判定归一化类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练Transformer通常位于损失景观的近似奇异极小值点，Fisher信息矩阵在"死方向"上退化。然而，定位这些死方向通常需要前向传播和激活特征分解，或基于采样的复杂度估计——没有一种方法能仅从网络参数直接计算。本文旨在寻找一种仅从参数即可读取的死方向，特别是针对LayerNorm Transformer。

Method: 作者通过代数推导证明：对于LayerNorm Transformer，LayerNorm仿射参数的逆缩放方向γ⁻¹/‖γ⁻¹‖是后最终归一化中心化激活协方差的精确代数核。该方向可通过三种等价形式计算：(1) γ的逆缩放；(2) LayerNorm投影矩阵P的特征向量；(3) 去除均值子空间的投影方向（RMSNorm无此子空间）。实验在14个预训练Transformer（9个LayerNorm，5个RMSNorm；160M-35B参数；涵盖语言和视觉任务）上进行验证：随机初始化时直接SVD验证预测方向与底部奇异方向的一致性；训练后检查点测量沿该方向的协方差特征值深化程度及新死方向的涌现。

Result: （1）随机初始化时，预测方向与实测底部奇异方向在所有9个LayerNorm模型上匹配至小数点后四位，5个RMSNorm模型正确识别为无此方向。（2）训练后，沿该方向的协方差特征值深化约10³倍，且更多死方向涌现；随机初始化到训练状态的差距可通过一次前向传播读取。（3）闭式推论一：残差流最小奇异值在13/14个模型上逐块保持，唯一例外Gemma4-31B被同一读取方法精准定位为真正死方向。（4）闭式推论二：核方向的存在性可仅凭参数判定Transformer的归一化类型。

Conclusion: 本文发现LayerNorm Transformer中γ⁻¹方向是一个可从参数直接读取的死方向，无需任何前向/反向计算。这一发现为分析预训练Transformer的奇异结构提供了极低成本工具，并揭示了LayerNorm特有的几何性质——均值减法投影器是产生该死方向的结构根源。

Abstract: Pretrained transformers sit near singular minima of the loss, where the Fisher information metric degenerates along dead directions: directions in parameter space along which the directional Fisher vanishes. Locating such a direction normally needs a forward pass and an eigendecomposition of activations, or a sampling-based complexity estimate; none returns a direction computable from the network's parameters alone. We give one, for LayerNorm transformers. The inverse-scale direction $γ^{-1}/\|γ^{-1}\|$ of the LayerNorm affine is an exact algebraic kernel of the post-final-norm centred activation covariance, for any input distribution, and induces a corresponding dead direction in parameter space. It is read from the LN scale parameter alone, with no forward or backward pass and no eigensolve: the cheapest dead-direction read, specific to LayerNorm. We test it on $14$ pretrained transformers ($9$ LayerNorm, $5$ RMSNorm; $160$M-$35$B; language and vision objectives). At random initialisation the predicted direction matches the measured bottom singular direction (one forward pass, direct SVD) to four decimal places on $9/9$ LayerNorm models, and is correctly absent on $5/5$ RMSNorm models, which lack the mean-subtraction projector that creates it. On the trained checkpoint the covariance eigenvalue along this direction deepens by ${\sim}10^3\times$ and further dead directions open; the random-init-to-trained gap is a one-forward-pass, per-checkpoint readout of singular structure along the predicted coordinate. Two consequences follow in closed form: the residual stream's smallest singular value is preserved block-to-block on $13/14$ transformers measured on their own input distribution, the one exception (Gemma$4$-$31$B) a genuine dead direction the same read pinpoints; and the kernel direction's presence classifies a transformer's normalisation from the parameters alone.

</details>


### [256] [Protein Representation Learning with Secondary-Structure and Energy-Filtered Hydrogen-Bond Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19374)
*Mohamed Mouhajir,Limei Wang,El Houcine Bergou,Hajar El Hammouti,Lamiae Azizi,Dongqi Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种二级结构感知的图神经网络（SSProNet），通过在蛋白质图中引入二级结构注释和基于氢键能量强度的边构建策略，增强了对蛋白质三维构象中局部结构背景和长程耦合的建模能力，在多个蛋白质基准任务上取得了优于现有图方法的性能，并提供了更强的生物学可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质图表示方法大多仅依赖序列邻接关系或几何邻近性，未能充分反映蛋白质折叠的核心原理——即蛋白质围绕α-螺旋和β-折叠等二级结构元素组织形成复杂三维构象，其中包含重复的局部基序和稳定的氢键相互作用。因此，需要一种能够显式建模二级结构信息和氢键拓扑的图神经网络。

Method: 作者提出SSProNet，其核心设计包括：（1）在残基级别的节点表示中融入二级结构（α-螺旋、β-折叠等）的赋值信息；（2）基于氢键相互作用构建图边，并利用能量强度进行过滤筛选，从而保留对蛋白质稳定性具有关键贡献的氢键连接；（3）最终图结构能够同时捕获局部结构上下文和远程空间耦合关系。

Result: 在常用的蛋白质基准数据集上，SSProNet相较于现有基于图的蛋白质表示方法取得了持续的、一致的性能提升。此外，所学到的图连接模式与已知的蛋白质结构基序高度吻合，验证了模型在生物学上的可解释性。

Conclusion: 将二级结构信息和能量过滤后的氢键拓扑融入图神经网络，为蛋白质表示学习提供了一种有效的归纳偏置。该方法不仅提升了预测性能，还使模型学习到的连通性具备生物学意义，为后续蛋白质建模研究提供了新方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: Graph-based representations are widely used in protein modeling, yet many existing approaches rely primarily on sequence adjacency or geometric proximity, which only partially reflect the principles governing protein folding. Proteins instead adopt complex three-dimensional conformations organized around secondary structure elements, such as $α$-helices and $β$-sheets, which encode recurring local motifs and stabilizing hydrogen-bond interactions. In this work, we introduce a secondary-structure-aware graph neural network for protein representation learning. Residue-level node representations are augmented with secondary structure assignments, and graph edges are constructed from hydrogen-bond interactions filtered by their energetic strength. This design enables the model to capture both local structural context and long-range couplings that are central to protein stability and function. We evaluate the proposed approach on commonly used protein benchmarks and observe consistent improvements over existing graph-based methods. In addition, the resulting graph representations offer enhanced biological interpretability, as the learned connectivity aligns with established structural motifs. These findings suggest that incorporating secondary structure and energy-filtered hydrogen-bond topology provides an effective inductive bias for protein representation learning. The code is released at https://github.com/mohamedmohamed2021/SSProNet

</details>


### [257] [Physics-Informed Discovery of Yield Functions in Plasticity via Convex Neural Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19375)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Donghyuk Cho,Jecheon Yu,Jeong Whan Yoon,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息驱动的框架，从全场位移和反力数据中直接发现各向异性屈服函数，无需应力观测、塑性应变测量或预设参数化形式。屈服函数以凸神经网络表示，嵌入弹塑性应力积分中，通过可微应力更新和力平衡损失训练，经有限元基准验证可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性屈服函数的识别面临三大挑战：屈服在力学测量中不可直接观测、方向标定需大量加载实验、以及选择适当的解析形式困难。现有方法依赖应力空间直接监督或预设函数形式，限制了灵活性和实用性。

Method: 将屈服函数表示为满足凸性、一阶正齐次性和拉压对称性的凸神经网络，嵌入可微弹塑性应力积分框架。训练时无需应力标签，而是通过多工况的全场位移和反力数据，构建物理信息力平衡损失函数，实现端到端学习。采用有限元基准（von Mises、Hill 1948、Yld2000-2d）进行验证，评估屈服轮廓一致性、位移噪声敏感性、塑性主动应力状态的可辨识性、认知不确定性和多项式代理部署。

Result: 该框架在三种屈服准则的有限元基准测试中成功恢复了屈服函数，屈服轮廓与真实函数高度一致。对位移噪声表现出鲁棒性，能够通过塑性主动应力状态实现可辨识性，并可部署为多项式代理模型以降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一条力学约束驱动的路径，能够从位移和反力数据中发现各向异性屈服函数，同时将识别出的本构组件保持在弹塑性应力积分的结构框架内，无需应力空间直接监督。

Abstract: Identifying anisotropic yield functions remains challenging since yielding is not directly observed in full-field mechanical measurements, directional calibration can require many loading directions, and selecting an appropriate analytical form is nontrivial. This study proposes a physics-informed framework for discovering yield functions from full-field displacement data and reaction force data, without stress observations, plastic strain measurements, direct yield surface data, or a prescribed parametric yield function. The framework identifies the yield function as a mechanically constrained constitutive component inside elastoplastic stress integration, rather than through direct stress-space supervision. The yield function is represented by a convex neural network that enforces convexity and positive homogeneity of degree one while imposing the assumed tension-compression symmetry, and this neural yield function is trained with a differentiable stress update and a physics-informed force equilibrium loss across multiple loading cases. The proposed framework is validated using finite element (FE) benchmark studies with von Mises, Hill 1948, and Yld2000-2d yield functions, assessing yield contour agreement, displacement-noise sensitivity, identifiability through plastically active stress states, epistemic uncertainty, and polynomial-surrogate deployment. This study provides a mechanics-constrained pathway for discovering anisotropic yield functions from displacement and force data while keeping the identified component within the structure of elastoplastic stress integration.

</details>


### [258] [Cost-Optimal LLM Routing with Limited User Feedback under User Satisfaction Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19376)
*Herbert Woisetschläger,Arastun Mammadli,Ryan Zhang,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLARouter 是一种在线LLM请求路由算法，利用生产环境中稀疏、单边的用户反馈，学习成本最优的路由策略，同时提供成本最优性和SLA合规性的理论保证，实验表明可降低高达2.2倍运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理成本快速增长，用户对响应质量有高要求（通常以SLA形式规定），现有成本感知的路由方法依赖完整反馈、离线训练、大量逐工作负载调优，且缺乏SLA保证或推理时自适应能力。

Method: 提出SLARouter在线路由算法，从生产系统中稀疏、单边的用户反馈信号中在线学习成本最优策略，并内置严格SLA合规保证。

Result: 在多种LLM基准测试中，SLARouter无需逐基准调优即可满足SLA约束，将运营成本相对现有基线降低最高2.2倍。

Conclusion: SLARouter有效解决了LLM推理场景中成本与质量之间的根本性矛盾，通过在线学习和理论保证实现了无需大量调优的高效路由。

Abstract: Inference costs for large language model (LLM) applications are rapidly growing, driven by surging demand and rising infrastructure cost. Users expect high-quality responses, and in commercial settings this is formally codified in Service Level Agreements (SLAs), creating a fundamental tension between cost and quality. Recent progress on cost-aware LLM request routing has shown potential to resolve this tension, but existing approaches rely on complete feedback signals, offline training, extensive per-workload tuning, and most lack SLA guarantees or inference-time adaptivity. We introduce SLARouter, an online routing algorithm that learns a cost-optimal policy from the sparse, one-sided user feedback available in production systems. SLARouter provides theoretical guarantees for both cost optimality and strict SLA compliance. Experiments across a wide range of LLM benchmarks show that SLARouter satisfies SLA constraints without the need for per-benchmark tuning, reducing operating cost by up to 2.2x over existing baselines.

</details>


### [259] [Emyx: Fast and efficient all-atom protein generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19377)
*Nicholas J. Williams,Ward Haddadin,Matteo P. Ferla,Constantin Schneider,Nicholas B. Woodall,Ruby Sedgwick,Christian D. Madsen,Andrew L. Hopkins,Edward O. Pyzer-Knapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Emyx 是一个仅 140M 参数的条件流匹配模型，用于计算酶设计。它用轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接取代了沉重的嵌入堆栈，并将流匹配插值重参数化到 EDM 噪声框架中。尽管规模最小，Emyx 在 AME 酶设计基准上全面超越了 Proteína-Complexa 和 RFdiffusion3，且训练仅需 682 GPU 小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前全原子生成模型继承了结构预测的昂贵架构，导致训练成本高、样本多样性有限。作者认为，对于仅依赖稀疏几何约束（而非丰富共进化信号）的生成器而言，这种复杂性是多余的，因此需要一种更轻量、高效的酶设计生成模型。

Method: 提出 Emyx，一个 140M 参数的条件流匹配模型：(1) 将模型容量集中在标准 Transformer 块中，用轻量级条件表示和稀疏连接替代沉重的嵌入堆栈；(2) 推导出流匹配插值到 EDM 噪声水平框架的精确重参数化，使流匹配训练效率与扩散模型的最先进采样方法相结合，无需重新训练。

Result: 在 AME 酶设计基准上，Emyx 在严格评估（要求全局折叠恢复和催化几何精度、结构新颖性、支架多样性、几何有效性）下的成功率均优于 Proteína-Complexa 和 RFdiffusion3，且训练仅需 682 GPU 小时，约为 RFdiffusion3 的 1/4。

Conclusion: 酶设计生成器不需要结构预测模型那样昂贵的架构；通过轻量化设计和流匹配与扩散采样框架的桥接，可以在大幅降低训练成本的同时实现更优的生成质量和多样性。

Abstract: Computational enzyme design requires generating proteins that scaffold catalytic residues and ligands, a task that demands both geometric accuracy and structural diversity from the underlying generative model. Current all-atom generators inherit expensive architectures from structure prediction, leading to high training costs and limited sample diversity. We argue that much of this complexity is unnecessary for generators, which condition on sparse geometric constraints rather than rich co-evolutionary signals. Emyx is a 140M-parameter conditional flow matching model that concentrates capacity within standard transformer blocks, replacing heavy embedding stacks with lightweight conditional representations and sparse connectivity. We additionally derive an exact reparametrisation of the flow matching interpolant into the EDM noise-level framework, bridging flow matching training efficiency with state-of-the-art sampling methods designed for diffusion models without retraining. Despite being the smallest model, Emyx outperforms both Proteína-Complexa and RFdiffusion3 against the AME enzyme design benchmark across success rate under strict evaluation requiring both global fold recovery and catalytic geometry accuracy, structural novelty, scaffold diversity, and geometric validity, while training in just $682$ GPU-hours, roughly $4\times$ less than RFdiffusion3.

</details>


### [260] [On the Oracle Complexity of Interpolation-Based Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19878)
*Dongmin Lee,William Lu,Anuran Makur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PPI-GD算法，通过在数据域等距采样构造多项式插值来近似全梯度，在损失函数对数据足够光滑且数据维度受限时，其oracle复杂度优于多种GD变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用ERM损失函数对训练数据（而非优化参数）的光滑性来提升梯度下降的oracle复杂度，是近期一阶优化器研究的新方向。本文旨在进一步探索该思路，设计一种通过数据域插值近似梯度的新方法。

Method: 提出分段多项式插值梯度下降（PPI-GD）：每次迭代中，在数据域的等距点上查询一阶oracle获取梯度样本，然后在适当大小的数据域分片上构造张量积多项式插值来近似全梯度。分析延伸了双三次样条插值的误差分析技术至d维张量积多项式插值场景。

Result: 当数据空间维度以训练样本数的多重对数函数为上界时，对于强凸和非凸损失函数，PPI-GD在损失函数足够光滑的关键区域中，其oracle复杂度优于若干GD变体。

Conclusion: PPI-GD通过挖掘数据域光滑性实现了oracle复杂度的改进，同时文中发展的d维张量积多项式插值误差分析工具具有独立的理论价值。

Abstract: Recent work on first-order optimizers for empirical risk minimization (ERM) has suggested that smoothness of ERM loss functions in the training data, rather than in the optimization parameters, can be leveraged to improve the oracle complexity of gradient descent (GD) methods. In this paper, we propose an inexact gradient method, piecewise polynomial interpolation-based gradient descent (PPI-GD), which approximates the full gradient in each iteration by querying the first-order oracle at equidistant points in the data domain to construct polynomial interpolants of the resulting gradient samples over appropriately sized patches of the data domain. We analyze the oracle complexity of PPI-GD for strongly convex and non-convex loss functions when the data space dimension is bounded by a polylogarithmic function of the number of training samples, and find it to outperform several GD variants in key regimes when the loss function is sufficiently smooth. Furthermore, our analysis extends several techniques from the error analysis of bicubic spline interpolants to the setting of $d$-variate tensor product polynomial interpolants which may be of independent interest in interpolation analysis.

</details>


### [261] [A Hybrid GNN-FEM Framework for Phase-Field Fracture Simulation. Physics-Preserving Hybridization for Generalizable Surrogate Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19378)
*Hyeonbin Moon,Yongjin Choi,Seunghwa Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种混合GNN-FEM框架用于相场断裂建模，用图神经网络替代传统交错格式中的相场更新步骤，保留FEM位移求解器，在保证物理一致性和历史依赖性的同时显著降低计算成本，并展现出跨几何、载荷、材料和离散化的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场法在模拟复杂裂纹演化方面具有优势，但需要求解耦合、非线性、历史依赖的系统，计算成本极高，限制了实际应用。需要一种既能加速计算又能保持物理一致性和泛化能力的替代方案。

Method: 提出混合GNN-FEM框架：(1) 保留传统交错格式中的FEM位移求解器以保证力学平衡和边界条件；(2) 用图神经网络(GNN)代理模型替换每个载荷增量步中的相场更新；(3) 通过无量纲特征设计、基于网格域的图构建、以及源自相场控制方程的物理信息损失函数，实现跨几何、载荷、材料和离散化的泛化。

Result: 数值实验表明，混合方法在保持与传统FEM相当的精度前提下显著降低了计算成本，并在不同几何形状、加载条件、材料属性和离散化设置下展现出鲁棒的预测性能。

Conclusion: 选择性代理策略聚焦于物理上有意义且增量结构化的学习目标，而非对整个断裂过程进行蛮力数据生成，这种增量一致的混合框架为非线性、历史依赖系统的科学机器学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning (SciML) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating simulations of complex physical systems, yet achieving physically consistent and generalizable predictions for nonlinear, history-dependent problems remains a central challenge. In this study, we propose a hybrid GNN--FEM framework for efficient and generalizable phase-field fracture modeling. While phase-field approaches provide a robust variational framework for simulating complex crack evolution, their high computational cost limits practical applications because they require solving coupled, nonlinear, and history-dependent systems within an incremental finite element procedure. To address this challenge, a graph neural network surrogate is integrated into the conventional staggered scheme, replacing the phase-field update at each load increment while retaining the FEM-based displacement solver to enforce mechanical equilibrium and boundary conditions. By preserving the incremental solution structure, the framework remains consistent with history-dependent fracture evolution without requiring the surrogate to approximate the full solution trajectory. This selective surrogate strategy emphasizes the identification of a physically meaningful and incrementally structured learning target, rather than relying on brute-force data generation to learn the full fracture process. The proposed framework achieves strong generalization across varying geometries, loading conditions, material properties, and discretizations through dimensionless feature design, a graph-based formulation on mesh-based domains, and a physics-informed loss derived from the governing phase-field equation. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the hybrid approach reduces computational cost while maintaining accuracy compared with conventional FEM, and exhibits robust predictive performance across diverse problem settings.

</details>


### [262] [Matching Markets meet Cumulative Prospect Theory: Towards Optimal and Adversarially Robust Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19883)
*Ananya Kunisetty,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了基于累积前景理论（CPT）的多智能体多臂老虎机问题，在双边匹配市场下提出算法，获得了玩家最优遗憾界 $\mathcal{O}(K\log T (1/\Delta)^{2/\alpha})$，并通过智能探索策略进一步优化为不依赖 $K$ 的改进界，同时扩展至对抗性腐败市场场景并建立了对数级遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体多臂老虎机研究通常假设理性决策者，忽略了现实中人类偏好的非线性与非理性特征。累积前景理论（CPT）在行为经济学中广泛用于建模人类决策，但将其纳入竞争性双边匹配市场下的多智能体学习算法尚未被充分探索。此外，现有遗憾界对最小偏好间隙 $\Delta$ 的依赖是次优的，且在臂数 $K$ 远大于玩家数 $N$ 时效率不足，同时缺乏对奖励被腐败的对抗性市场环境的鲁棒性分析。

Method: 采用累积前景理论（CPT）框架，通过 $\alpha$-Hölder 连续权重函数对智能体奖励进行非线性扭曲，建模人类偏好。首先分析基于CPT扭曲奖励的最先进学习算法，推导玩家遗憾界。随后，通过在探索阶段智能选择活跃臂集（active set），消除主导项中对 $K$ 的依赖，在 $K \gg N$ 场景下获得改进遗憾界。进一步，针对对抗性市场，在腐败预算已知和未知两种情况下，分别提出并分析以CPT为风险敏感度量的鲁棒算法，建立对数级玩家最优遗憾保证。

Result: 1. 基于CPT扭曲奖励的学习算法获得玩家最优遗憾界 $\mathcal{O}(K\log T (1/\Delta)^{2/\alpha})$；2. 通过活跃臂集策略改进遗憾界，在主导项中移除对 $K$ 的依赖，当 $K \gg N$ 时实现最优遗憾；3. 在腐败预算已知和未知的对抗性市场中，均建立了对数级玩家最优遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 将累积前景理论引入多智能体多臂老虎机竞争性双边匹配问题中，成功建模了人类非线性偏好，并通过活跃臂集策略和鲁棒算法设计，分别在标准场景和对抗性腐败场景下获得了改进的、最优的对数级玩家遗憾界，为以人为中心的决策系统提供了理论保障。

Abstract: We study a multi-agent multi-armed bandit problem in the competitive setup with two-sided matching markets under a human centric decision making model. To capture human preferences, we use cumulative prospect theory (CPT) that weighs the actions of the agent in a nonlinear fashion using a ($α$-Hölder continuous) weight function. CPT has been widely used in behavioral economics and risk sensitive machine learning to emulate human preferences. We analyze the state-of-the-art learning algorithm with CPT weight distorted rewards and obtain a player optimal regret of $\mathcal{O}(K\log T \left(\frac{1}Δ\right)^{2/α})$, where $K$ denotes the number of arms, $T$ is the learning horizon, and $Δ$ represents (suitably defined) players' minimum preference gap. Noticing the dependence on $Δ$ to be sub-optimal, we further improve this regret by judiciously selecting the active set of arms during exploration, which removes the dependence on $K$ in the dominant term and achieves an improved (optimal) regret guarantees in the setting where the number of arms $K$ is significantly larger than the number of players $N$. In addition, we consider adversarial markets where the observed rewards of the agents may be corrupted. We propose and analyze algorithms for robust markets with CPT as risk sensitive measure in both settings where the total corruption budget is known and where it is unknown, and establish logarithmic player-optimal regret guarantees in both cases.

</details>


### [263] [How Linear Is a Transformer Feed-Forward Block? Per-Block Linear Recoverability Is Learned, Not Architectural](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19379)
*Stuart Whipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需优化器的线性可恢复性度量指标 R²_lin，用于量化 Transformer FFN 块的非线性程度。研究发现 FFN 的线性度在不同模型和不同层之间高度异质，且由训练习得而非架构决定；该指标还可用于指导模型压缩，并揭示了线性基线拟合中的方法学陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 的前馈网络（FFN）通常被视为存储非线性计算的关键组件，但很少有研究实际量化一个训练好的 FFN 块到底有多"非线性"。作者希望填补这一空白，提供一种可靠且无需训练的度量方法，以理解 FFN 中线性与非线性计算的实际分布。

Method: 将每个 FFN 视为位置级的输入-输出映射，将其分解为精确的最小二乘线性近似加上残差。定义 R²_lin 为线性映射所能解释的保留方差比例，作为该块线性可恢复性的度量。此外，使用低秩双线性探针分析残差结构，并利用该度量指导单层替换压缩实验。

Result: 在 GPT-2、Pythia-160m 和 LLaMA-160m 的全部 12 层中，R²_lin 高度异质且随深度非单调变化，范围从近乎线性（>0.99）到强非线性（<0.3）。激活函数（GELU）相同的 GPT-2 和 Pythia-160m 呈现截然不同的 R²_lin 分布，表明线性度是习得属性而非架构属性。低秩双线性探针仅能恢复残差中少量的 R²，其增益与残差非线性程度不相关，表明未恢复的计算是高阶或分布式结构。高可恢复性块可用单层线性替换（GPT-2 早期 FFN 以 8 倍参数减少换 +0.77 困惑度），而低可恢复性块则不适合此类压缩。此外，训练线性基线在病态条件的 Transformer 激活上可能严重欠收敛，因此作者使用精确闭式解作为理论上限。

Conclusion: FFN 的非线性程度是一种习得的、逐块变化的属性，R²_lin 提供了可靠且计算成本极低的度量手段。该指标不仅能解释模型内部的计算结构（非线性并非单一的位置级乘积），还具有实用价值：可作为模型压缩的安全信号，并纠正了训练线性探针中的方法学缺陷。

Abstract: Transformer feed-forward networks (FFNs) are often treated as nonlinear stores of computation, yet how nonlinear a trained FFN block actually is has rarely been measured. We treat each FFN as a position-wise input-to-output map and split it into the exact least-squares linear approximation plus a residual. The held-out variance the closed-form linear map explains defines a block's linear recoverability (R^2_lin), an optimiser-free measure of its linearity.
  Across all twelve blocks of GPT-2, Pythia-160m, and llama-160m, R^2_lin is highly heterogeneous and non-monotone with depth, ranging from near-linear (>0.99) to strongly nonlinear (<0.3) between adjacent blocks, and is not set by the activation function: same-width GELU models GPT-2 and Pythia-160m have sharply different profiles, so recoverability is a learned property of individual trained blocks, not an architectural one. A low-rank bilinear probe of the residual recovers only a few points of R^2, with gain uncorrelated with residual nonlinearity: the unrecovered computation is not a single position-wise product but higher-order or distributed structure.
  The measurement also serves as a targeted compression signal: recoverable blocks admit large single-layer replacements (GPT-2's early FFN at 8x fewer parameters for +0.77 perplexity), while low-recoverability blocks flag where this is unsafe. It further exposes a methodological pitfall: trained linear baselines can badly under-converge on ill-conditioned transformer activations, so we report the exact closed-form least-squares ceiling throughout.

</details>


### [264] [Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20557)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了多校准(multicalibration)领域的一个核心开放问题：证明了确定性预测器可以达到极小极大最优的样本复杂度，推翻了此前"随机化是达到最优样本复杂度必要条件"的普遍认知，并将结果推广到结果不可区分性(OI)、全能预测器(omnipredictors)和泛预测器(panpredictors)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多校准是可信机器学习的基本要求，但此前所有达到极小极大最优样本复杂度 Õ(ε⁻³) 的预测器都是随机化的，确定性预测器的样本复杂度显著更差。随机化是否为多校准最优样本复杂度的必要条件，是[CLNR26]明确提出的开放问题，也是多项前期工作隐含的问题。

Method: 作者给出了一个极小极大最优的多校准算法，该算法输出确定性预测器。随后将算法推广到产生满足结果不可区分性(Outcome Indistinguishability, OI)的最优确定性预测器，适用于有限或有限覆盖的检验集合。

Result: 成功构造了达到 Õ(ε⁻³) 极小极大最优样本复杂度的确定性多校准预测器，证明随机化并非必要。进一步得到了具有最优样本复杂度的确定性全能预测器和泛预测器，解决了[OKK25]和[BHHLZ25]提出的开放问题。

Conclusion: 确定性算法可以在多校准及相关领域（OI、全能预测器、泛预测器）达到与随机化算法相同的最优样本复杂度，根本性地回答了"随机化是否必要"这一开放问题，为可信机器学习提供了更强的理论基础。

Abstract: A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works.
  We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].

</details>


### [265] [VERITAS: Verifier-Guided Proof Search for Zero-Shot Formal Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19399)
*Manish Acharya,Zhenyu Liao,Yueke Zhang,Kevin Leach,Yu Huang,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERITAS 是一个零样本框架，通过两阶段协议（Best-of-N 采样 + 基于批评的 MCTS）将验证器的所有反馈信号（语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）重新引入证明搜索，在 miniF2F 上达到 40.6%，并发布了组合数学基准 VERITAS-CombiBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的形式化证明器通常将验证器产生的丰富信号（如语法错误、类型不匹配、部分目标进展）粗粒度地压缩为二元的通过/失败信号，导致大量有用的反馈信息在证明搜索过程中被浪费。作者旨在充分利用这些被忽视的验证器反馈来改进证明搜索。

Method: 提出 VERITAS 两阶段协议：(1) Phase 1 进行 Best-of-N 采样，收集失败案例作为显式负例；(2) Phase 2 采用基于批评（critic-guided）的 MCTS 搜索，将 Phase 1 的验证器反馈信号（语法错误、类型错误、目标进展等）路由回搜索过程，引导树搜索向更有希望的方向探索。Phase 2 保证不会丢失 Phase 1 已解决的定理，因此额外解出的定理可归因于反馈驱动的探索。

Result: 在 miniF2F 上达到 40.6%（独立运行的 Best-of-5 为 36.9%，Portfolio 为 26.2%）；在新发布的 55 道组合数学基准 VERITAS-CombiBench 上达到 7.3%（Best-of-5 仅 1.8%，低于 Portfolio 的 3.6%），表明当需要从验证器反馈中迭代恢复正确引理名称时，无引导采样反而有害。

Conclusion: 将验证器的丰富反馈信号系统地路由回证明搜索过程，能显著提升 LLM 形式化证明的性能，尤其在需要迭代纠错的复杂场景下，反馈驱动探索比盲目采样更有效。

Abstract: LLM-based formal provers often collapse rich verifier signals (syntax errors, type mismatches, partial goal progress) into a binary pass/fail bit. We present VERITAS, a zero-shot framework that routes every verifier signal back into proof search through a two-phase protocol: Best-of-N sampling first, then a critic-guided MCTS pass that ingests Phase 1 failures as explicit negative examples. The protocol preserves every theorem solved by its own Phase 1 sweep, so Phase 2's additional solves are attributable to feedback-driven exploration. VERITAS reaches 40.6% on miniF2F (vs. an independently run Best-of-5 at 36.9%, Portfolio 26.2%) and 7.3% on VERITAS-CombiBench, a 55-theorem combinatorics benchmark we release on which Best-of-5 (1.8%) falls below Portfolio (3.6%), exposing that unguided sampling hurts when correct lemma names must be recovered iteratively from verifier feedback. Artifacts are available on GitHub.

</details>


### [266] [Thermodynamic Signatures of Reasoning: Free-Energy and Spectral-Form-Factor Diagnostics for Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19404)
*Salim Khazem*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Free-Energy Signatures (Fes)，将每层注意力拉普拉斯矩阵视为哈密顿量，提取其热力学势（配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容）及随机矩阵理论（RMT）谱形状因子，用于 LLM 幻觉检测。理论证明了 Fes 的 Lipschitz 稳定性、表达力及 PAC 边界；实验在 6 个模型和 6 个基准上取得最优 AUROC，无监督设定下 RMT 偏差分数也表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于注意力图拉普拉斯谱的幻觉检测方法仅使用少数特征值或手工标量来概括谱结构，浪费了大部分谱信息。需要一种更充分地利用拉普拉斯谱结构的描述子，以提升幻觉检测性能。

Method: 提出 Fes：将每层注意力拉普拉斯矩阵视为哈密顿量，提取配分函数、自由能、谱熵、热容等热力学势，以及 RMT 谱形状因子作为谱描述子。基于 Fes 构建无需训练的检测器（轻量级探针），并给出了无监督的 RMT 偏差分数检测方案。

Result: 在 6 个开源 LLM 和 6 个基准上，基于 Fes 的轻量探针取得聚合 AUROC 最优，平均比 LapEig 高 +6.5 点，比 GoR-4 高 +2.4 点。无监督 RMT 偏差分数达到均值 AUROC 0.71。RMT 分析表明正确生成呈现 Wigner-Dyson 谱统计，幻觉则更接近 Poisson 统计。

Conclusion: Fes 作为一种丰富、稳定且表达力强的谱描述子，显著提升了基于注意力谱的幻觉检测性能，且无需更新底层 LLM。RMT 视角为理解幻觉的谱特征提供了新洞见。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) is deployment-critical, and recent work shows that the spectrum of attention-derived graph Laplacians carries strong signal about reasoning quality. Prior spectral diagnostics, however, summarize the Laplacian spectrum by a handful of eigenvalues or hand-picked scalars, leaving most of its structure unused. We propose Free-Energy Signatures (Fes), a spectral descriptor that treats each layer's attention Laplacian as a Hamiltonian and extracts its thermodynamic potentials partition function, free energy, spectral entropy, heat capacity together with the random-matrix-theory (RMT) spectral form factor. We prove three results: (i)~Lipschitz stability of Fes under attention perturbation; (ii)~an expressiveness result showing that Fes enriches finite spectral summaries and approximates moment-derived spectral functionals under explicit regularity and grid-resolution assumptions; and (iii)~a finite-sample PAC bound on the AUROC of a training-free detector built from Fes. Empirically, across six open-weight LLMs and six benchmarks, a lightweight probe on Fes descriptors achieves the strongest aggregate AUROC among attention-spectral baselines, improving over LapEig by $+6.5$ AUROC points and over GoR-4 by $+2.4$ points on average, while requiring no update to the underlying LLM. In the fully unsupervised setting, an RMT-deviation score achieves mean AUROC $0.71$, providing a label-free but weaker detector. A complementary RMT analysis shows that correct generations exhibit more Wigner-Dyson like spectral statistics, whereas hallucinations exhibit more Poisson-like statistics. The anonymized code and config are provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [267] [FlexLAM: Resolving the Bottleneck Trade-off in Latent Action Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19408)
*Takanori Yoshimoto,Yang Hu,Naruya Kondo,Tatsuya Matsushima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlexLAM 提出用可变长度潜动作替代固定容量瓶颈，通过嵌套 dropout 训练前缀有效码，在不改变架构和损失函数的前提下，统一了不同 token 预算下的潜动作建模，并在稀缺标签和低回报对齐压力测试中全面匹配或超越独立训练的固定容量 LAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜动作模型 (LAM) 采用固定容量瓶颈，存在瓶颈权衡困境：瓶颈过紧会丢失动作对齐所需的过渡线索；瓶颈过松则保留额外过渡变异，在标签稀缺或分布狭窄时难以消解。作者希望打破这种固定容量的限制，实现按需分配编码容量。

Method: FlexLAM 采用嵌套 dropout (nested dropout) 训练可变长度潜动作，生成前缀有效码 (prefix-valid codes)：代码的前缀子序列本身构成有效的低容量表示。训练时对潜码维度施加有序的 dropout，迫使模型将最重要的过渡结构编码在靠前的维度中，后续维度逐步补充细节。无需修改架构或引入新损失函数，推理时可直接调整 token 预算而无需重新训练。

Result: 单个 FlexLAM 在标准稀缺标签监督和低回报单任务对齐压力测试下，在每个评估的 token 预算点上均匹配或超越独立训练的固定容量 LAM；在 Ego4D 过渡重建任务上也取得提升。表明 FlexLAM 不仅在推理时可调节，而且在同等 token 预算下学到了更优的潜动作接口。

Conclusion: 可变长度潜动作是对潜动作模型、潜动作世界模型及视频预训练动作接口中固定容量瓶颈的无架构侵入式即插即用升级方案。

Abstract: Latent actions provide a compact interface between action-free video and downstream decision-making, yet existing Latent Action Models (LAMs) force every transition through a fixed-capacity bottleneck. We identify a bottleneck trade-off: overly tight codes can discard transition cues needed for action alignment, while overly loose codes preserve additional transition variation that must be resolved when alignment labels are scarce or narrowly distributed. FlexLAM replaces this fixed capacity with variable-length latent actions trained by nested dropout, yielding prefix-valid codes that capture compact transition structure first and add detail only when needed, without new architectures or losses. A single FlexLAM matches or surpasses separately trained fixed-capacity LAMs at every evaluated token budget under standard scarce-label supervision and under a low-return single-task alignment stress test, indicating that FlexLAM is not merely adjustable at inference time but learns a better latent-action interface at the same token budgets. The same model supports inference-time token-budget adjustment without retraining, and FlexLAM improves Ego4D transition reconstruction. These results suggest that variable-length latent actions are an architecture-free, drop-in upgrade to the fixed-capacity bottleneck in latent action models, latent-action world models, and video-pretrained action interfaces.

</details>


### [268] [Spectral DPPs via NEPv: A Scalable Continuous Relaxation of Determinantal MAP for Diversity-Aware Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19411)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将DPP的MAP推断（NP-hard）转化为Stiefel流形上的连续优化问题，推导出一类新型非线性特征值问题（NEPv），并提出基于自洽场（SCF）迭代的求解器，实现近线性时间复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPP的MAP子集选择在数据筛选、主动学习、提示选择等场景中至关重要，但现有贪婪/采样算法对地面集大小n呈超线性扩展，无法应对数百万到数十亿规模的数据集。

Method: 将DPP-MAP重构为Stiefel流形上的连续优化，证明一阶最优性条件构成一种新型NEPv（带特征向量依赖的非线性特征值问题），并设计自洽场（SCF）迭代求解，利用谱间隙给出局部收缩保证。算法仅需核矩阵-向量乘积，复杂度O((ndk+nk²)·t)。

Result: 所得算法SCF迭代具有基于谱间隙的局部收缩保证，每次迭代仅需矩阵-向量乘积，对n近线性扩展，并兼容低秩核和特征映射核。

Conclusion: 通过连续松弛和NEPv-SCF求解器，DPP-MAP可扩展至大规模数据场景，理论和扩展性分析已完备，实际数据基准测试留待后续实证研究。

Abstract: Selecting a small, diverse, high-quality subset from a massive pool of candidates is a recurring primitive in modern machine learning -- data curation and coreset selection for training and fine-tuning large models, active-learning batch acquisition, prompt and exemplar selection for in-context learning, retrieval diversification, and experimental design. Determinantal Point Processes (\DPP s) give a principled, well-calibrated notion of diversity for this task, but their \emph{MAP} objective -- pick a size-$k$ subset $S$ maximizing $\logdet(L_S)$ -- is NP-hard, and the standard greedy and sampling algorithms scale superlinearly in the ground-set size $n$. This cost is prohibitive precisely in the data-centric regime where diversity matters most, where $n$ ranges over millions to billions of candidate examples, features, or embeddings. We recast \DPP-MAP as a continuous optimization problem over the Stiefel manifold, and show that its first-order optimality conditions form a \emph{Nonlinear Eigenvalue Problem with eigenvector dependency} (\NEPv) of a previously unstudied form. This \NEPv\ admits a self-consistent field (\SCF) iteration with a spectral-gap-based local contraction guarantee, giving a principled iterative solver where the diversity objective drives an eigenvector-dependent operator. The resulting algorithm, \OurMethod, requires only matrix-vector products with the kernel and runs in time $O\!\big((ndk+nk^2)\,t\big)$ for a small number of iterations $t$, scaling near-linearly in $n$ and integrating directly with low-rank and feature-map kernels common in ML. This paper focuses on the relaxation, solver, and scaling analysis; full real-data benchmarking is left to a planned empirical study.

</details>


### [269] [Spectral Retrieval-Augmented Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19412)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecReTF 是一种面向非平稳时间序列预测的检索增强方法，通过将时间序列转换为窗口化频域表示，结合振幅与相位信息的相似性度量，并采用指数移动平均加权强调近期模式，解决了现有检索方法的光谱盲区和时间等权问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强时间序列预测方法存在两个核心缺陷：(1) 光谱盲区——忽略频域中反映周期性结构的振幅与相位特征；(2) 时间等权——对历史数据一视同仁，未能突出近期模式的更高相关性。这导致它们在面对复杂、非平稳时间序列时性能受限。

Method: SpecReTF 提出三大技术组件：(1) 将时间序列转换为窗口化频率表示，同时编码振幅和相位信息；(2) 设计融合振幅与相位的联合相似性度量，在频域中匹配历史模式；(3) 引入指数移动平均加权方案，赋予近期窗口更高权重，以平衡时效性与历史上下文。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，SpecReTF 优于传统时域检索方法，在多样化非平稳时间序列上取得了更优的预测精度。

Conclusion: SpecReTF 通过频域检索与时间加权机制，有效弥补了现有方法的不足，为检索增强时间序列预测提供了新的技术范式。

Abstract: Time series forecasting leverages historical patterns to predict future values, but traditional methods face challenges when dealing with complex, non-stationary patterns that are difficult to memorize during training. Retrieval-augmented approaches have emerged as promising solutions by retrieving similar historical patterns to enhance predictions. However, existing retrieval methods suffer from two fundamental limitations: spectral blindness, which overlooks critical frequency-domain characteristics that capture underlying periodic structures, and temporal recency, which treats all historical data equally without emphasizing recent, more relevant patterns. In this paper, we propose SpecReTF, a novel retrieval method that addresses these issues by converting time series into windowed frequency representations, measuring similarity with a combined metric that captures both amplitude and phase information. To balance recency and historical context, we apply an exponential moving average weighting scheme that emphasizes recent windows. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that SpecReTF outperforms time-domain retrieval methods, achieving superior forecasting accuracy across diverse, non-stationary time series.

</details>


### [270] [Does Text Actually Help? Uncovering and Resolving Text Collapse in Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19413)
*Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Dung Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了多模态时间序列预测中的"文本坍缩"问题——文本分支退化为与内容无关的变换，并提出REST-TS框架，通过让文本分支专门监督数值主干无法解释的残差，强制其从文本中提取真正有用的信息，从而在多个领域取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态时间序列预测框架存在"文本坍缩"现象：由于数值输入与输出之间存在强自相关性，数值主干天然占主导地位，而文本分支尽管携带互补且关键的信息，却未被充分利用，退化为几乎无判别力的变换。研究者旨在解决这一根本性的不对称问题，迫使文本分支真正利用文本内容。

Method: 提出REST-TS（Residual-Exclusive Supervision for Text in Time Series）框架。核心设计原则是将不对称性转化为优势：数值主干独立产出数值预测，文本分支则被专门监督以预测残差中数值无法解释的结构化成分。由于没有任何数值通路可以降低这些残差损失，文本分支被迫从输入描述中提取真正的内容信息。

Result: 在多个真实世界领域和不同骨干架构上进行了评估，REST-TS取得了最先进的性能表现，并一致地展现出比现有框架更高的文本分支利用率，为"在残差上监督文本分支能迫使其提取真实文本内容"这一设计理念提供了强有力的实证支持。

Conclusion: 文本坍缩是多模态时间序列预测中的关键失败模式，根源在于数值与输出的强自相关性导致文本分支欠利用。REST-TS通过将文本分支绑定到数值模型无法解释的残差信号上，有效逆转了这一困境，验证了残差专有监督策略在实现文本分支真正参与预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal time series forecasting, which pairs numerical sequences with domain-relevant textual reports, promises to inject world knowledge into forecasting pipelines. However, we uncover a critical failure mode in existing frameworks that we term text collapse: the text branch converges to a content-independent transformation, contributing negligible discriminative signal regardless of the input description. We argue that text collapse is a consequence of a fundamental asymmetry in time series forecasting: the numerical input is strongly autocorrelated with the output, making the numerical backbone inherently dominant, while the text branch, despite carrying complementary and often critical information, is insufficiently utilized, leading to its systematic underexploitation. To address this, we propose \textbf{REST-TS} (\textbf{R}esidual-\textbf{E}xclusive \textbf{S}upervision for \textbf{T}ext in \textbf{T}ime \textbf{S}eries), which turns the asymmetry into a design principle: the numerical backbone produces its own independent numerical forecast, and the text branch is exclusively supervised to predict the structured components of the residual, the prediction gap that numbers cannot explain. Because no numerical pathway can reduce these losses, the text branch must extract genuine content from the input description. Evaluated across diverse real-world domains and backbone architectures, REST-TS achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently demonstrates greater text-branch utilization than existing frameworks, providing strong empirical evidence that supervising the text branch on the residual compels it to extract genuine content from the input.

</details>


### [271] [MortarBench: Evaluating Mortgage Loan Origination Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19416)
*Matthew Toles,Yunan Lu,Manav Munjal,Bojun Liu,Yuanhao Deng,Stephanie Selig,Derek Rindner,Cheng Li,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MortarBench是一个面向贷款发放智能体的基准测试，通过金融数据合成与变异管线生成覆盖真实世界分布的测试样例。现有最先进的LLM在该基准上表现不佳（闭源模型最高77.1%精确匹配准确率），且存在对外国姓名的系统性偏见。作者提出CRIT置信度校准框架，将准确率提升至80.5%，同时改善了风险管理的可控性并降低了偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界已开始使用贷款发放智能体辅助人工，但缺乏公开基准来评估这些智能体的表现。填补这一空白是构建MortarBench的核心动机，同时也希望揭示LLM在金融场景中的能力短板与偏见问题。

Method: 首先构建MortarBench基准，采用金融数据合成与变异管线（synthesis and mutation pipeline）生成具有广泛边缘案例覆盖且匹配真实世界数据分布的问题。然后在此基准上评估主流LLM的表现。针对发现的准确率不足和偏见问题，提出CRIT（confidence calibration framework）置信度校准框架，通过对模型输出的置信度进行校准来提升准确率、改善风险管理和减少偏见。

Result: (1) 闭源SOTA模型在MortarBench上精确匹配准确率最高仅77.1%；(2) LLM对非英文姓名存在系统性的"外来性"感知偏见；(3) CRIT框架将准确率提升至80.5%，并在风险管理的导向性和减少偏见方面均有改善。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在贷款发放这一金融场景中尚不具备可靠替代人类的能力，存在准确率不足和系统性偏见等关键缺陷。通过置信度校准可以部分缓解这些问题，但仍需进一步研究以推动贷款发放智能体的实际部署。

Abstract: Loan origination is the process by which a lender creates a new loan, from application and underwriting through approval and funding. This process serves a critical role in evaluating the eligibility and level of risk posed by an applicant. Recently, firms have begun using mortgage loan agents to augment human loan officers, despite a lack of any public benchmark. To fill this gap, we present MortarBench, a loan origination agent benchmark. MortarBench uses a financial data synthesis and mutation pipeline to generate examples with broad edge case coverage that match real-world distributions and questions. We find that state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) perform poorly, with closed-source models achieving at most 77.1\% exact match accuracy. We also discover systematic biases in LLM perception of foreignness related to non-English names. Noting these weaknesses, we introduce CRIT, a confidence calibration framework. Our method increases accuracy to 80.5\% while improving risk management steering and reducing bias.

</details>


### [272] [3D-DLP: Self-Supervised 3D Object-Centric Scene Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19451)
*Ellina Zhang,Madhaven Iyengar,Amir Zadeh,Chuan Li,Deepak Pathak,David Held,Tal Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 3D-DLP 是一种自监督的、以对象为中心的表征学习模型，将场景级 RGB-D 或体素观测分解为一组三维隐粒子，每个粒子编码解耦的 3D 关键点位置、边界框尺寸及外观特征，并通过重构目标学习可解释的逐粒子分割图，在下游机器人操作任务中优于缺乏显式 3D 信息或依赖密集 3D 输入的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以对象为中心的表征学习方法大多局限于 2D 空间，缺乏显式的三维结构理解；而机器人操作等下游任务迫切需要紧凑、可解释且具备 3D 几何信息的对象级表征，同时避免直接使用内存密集型的密集 3D 输入。因此需要一种能够从 3D 观测中自监督地发现对象实体并编码其空间属性的方法。

Method: 基于 Deep Latent Particles (DLP) 框架，3D-DLP 将场景分解为一组 3D 隐粒子，每个粒子解耦编码关键点 3D 位置、边界框尺寸和外观特征。模型通过端到端的自监督重构目标进行训练，同时学习可解释的逐粒子分割图。输入支持 RGB-D 或体素观测，通过操纵粒子位置并解码可实现新场景配置的生成。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界数据集上，3D-DLP 学到的隐空间具有可解释性和可控性：通过操控粒子位置可生成新的场景配置。在机器人操作下游任务中，利用 3D-DLP 的紧凑 3D 隐粒子表征，性能优于两类基线——缺乏显式 3D 信息的方法，以及依赖内存密集型密集 3D 输入但缺乏对象中心结构的方法。

Conclusion: 3D-DLP 成功将对象中心表征学习从 2D 拓展到 3D，实现了紧凑、解耦且可解释的 3D 场景表征，在下游机器人操作任务中验证了其实用价值，为 3D 场景理解与机器人学习的结合提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce 3D-DLP, a self-supervised object-centric representation learning model that decomposes scene-level RGB-D or voxel observations into a set of 3D latent particles. Building on the Deep Latent Particles (DLP) framework, each particle encodes disentangled attributes, including 3D keypoint position, bounding box dimensions, and appearance features, and represents a distinct entity in the scene. The model learns interpretable per-particle segmentation maps through an end-to-end self-supervised reconstruction objective. We demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that the learned latent space is interpretable and controllable: by manipulating particle positions and decoding, we can generate novel scene configurations. Furthermore, we show that leveraging these compact 3D latent particles for downstream robotic manipulation improves performance over baselines that either lack explicit 3D information or rely on memory-intensive dense 3D inputs without object-centric structure. Code and videos are available at https://eubooks3003.github.io/3d-dlp.

</details>


### [273] [Can In-Context Learning Support Intrinsic Curiosity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19476)
*Eric Elmoznino,Sangnie Bhardwaj,Johannes von Oswald,Rajai Nasser,Blaise Agüera y Arcas,João Sacramento,Rif A. Saurous,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨能否利用序列模型的上下文学习能力作为免更新的世界模型，训练探索策略以最大化"学习进度"内在奖励。作者证明在一般马尔可夫决策过程中，无偏地实现这一点是不可能的，但在非时序设置（如主动学习与贝叶斯实验设计）中，ICL衍生的奖励可渐近收敛于真实学习进度，并通过实验验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模序列模型虽已革新数据建模，但自动化数据选择（即"内在好奇心"）仍是一大挑战。经典方法通过衡量新观测对世界模型预测能力的提升（即"学习进度"）来奖励探索，但评估这些奖励需要在每个轨迹内进行昂贵的梯度下降内循环，难以大规模应用。本文旨在探究序列模型的涌现上下文学习能力是否能消除这一计算瓶颈。

Method: 作者首先从理论上分析在一般马尔可夫决策过程中，仅使用上下文学习器的预测误差和反事实上下文操作来无偏估计学习进度的可行性。进而，针对非时序设置（包括主动学习和贝叶斯实验设计）这一重要子类，证明ICL衍生的奖励可以下界并渐近收敛于真实学习进度。最后，在连续和符号环境中进行受控实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明：（1）在一般MDP中，基于ICL预测误差的内在奖励要么存在干扰项导致有偏估计，要么无法实现；（2）在非时序设置中，ICL衍生奖励成功下界并渐近收敛于真实学习进度。实验上，所提出的ICL驱动框架成功训练出能最优探索的好奇心驱动数据收集策略。

Conclusion: 序列模型的上下文学习能力可在特定条件下（非时序主动学习场景）作为高效的内在奖励机制，消除传统学习进度方法中昂贵的梯度更新内循环。但在一般MDP中存在根本性限制，无法无偏实现。该工作为自动化好奇心驱动探索提供了新的理论视角与实用框架。

Abstract: Effective machine learning depends not only on how we model data, but also on what data we choose to collect. While large sequence models have revolutionized data modeling, the problem of automated data selection, or "intrinsic curiosity", remains a significant challenge. Classic approaches incentivize exploration by rewarding an agent based on its "learning progress", which measures how much a newly acquired observation improves a world model's predictive ability. However, evaluating these rewards traditionally requires expensive inner loops of gradient descent updates within each trajectory, rendering them computationally impractical at scale. In this work, we investigate whether the emergent in-context learning (ICL) capabilities of sequence models can eliminate this bottleneck by serving as immediate, update-free world models. Specifically, we evaluate whether an exploration policy can be trained to maximize learning progress, using solely the prediction errors and counterfactual context manipulations of an in-context learner. We first prove that in general Markov decision processes, this is in fact impossible in an unbiased way: the resulting intrinsic rewards either suffer from nuisance terms that bias their estimation of true learning progress, or they cannot be implemented using an in-context learner's prediction errors. Conversely, we prove a positive result for a broad subclass of non-temporal settings, encompassing active learning and Bayesian Experimental Design: here, ICL-derived rewards successfully bound and asymptotically converge to the true learning progress. We corroborate our theory with controlled experiments across continuous and symbolic environments, demonstrating that our ICL-driven framework successfully trains curious data-collection policies that explore optimally.

</details>


### [274] [Insulin4RL: Real-Time Insulin Management in the Intensive Care Unit for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19481)
*Thomas Frost,Steve Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Insulin4RL 是一个基于 MIMIC-IV 构建的医疗离线强化学习数据集，包含 ICU 中 12,209 名患者超过 375,000 条胰岛素输注滴定决策记录。该数据集的突出特点是保留了临床实践中自然产生的不规则时间间隔输入与动作，而非传统方法中的人工离散化处理，旨在为真实临床采样假设下的离线 RL 研究提供更可靠的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医疗离线强化学习研究普遍将 EHR 数据强制离散化为固定、均匀的时间间隔，这种做法人为构建了虚构的临床场景表征，损害了回顾性模型评估的泛化能力。因此，需要构建一个保留自然不规则采样特性的真实临床决策数据集，以弥合离线 RL 模型训练评估与真实临床环境之间的差距。

Method: 作者从 MIMIC-IV 数据库中提取 ICU 内需要胰岛素输注滴定的患者队列，保留其原始的不规则时间序列数据（即非等间隔的观测与干预记录），构建 Insulin4RL 数据集；随后采用无模型离线强化学习方法建立基线性能指标，并引入拟合 Q 评估（FQE）作为标准化评估协议。

Result: 构建了包含 12,209 名患者、超过 375,000 条标注决策的不规则时间序列离线 RL 数据集；提供了数据集结构与特征描述，以及基于无模型离线 RL 的基线性能指标和 FQE 标准化评估协议。

Conclusion: Insulin4RL 为在真实临床采样假设下研究离线 RL 模型性能提供了重要资源，并指出了未来可利用该数据集开展的研究方向。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (ORL) offers the potential to improve the quality of clinical decision-making using historical electronic health record (EHR) data.
